+// #321 native s16: extract a 16-bit compile-time constant from an operand, if it
+// is one. Handles both forms a 16-bit literal takes by the time it reaches the
+// selector: a direct (look-through) constant, and — the common case — a
+// G_MERGE_VALUES of two byte constants (a 16-bit immediate is split into lo/hi
+// bytes before selection; getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough does NOT look
+// through the merge, so reconstruct it here). Used to fold `var OP const` into
+// the *Imm16 forms instead of materializing the constant into an Imag16 pair.
+static std::optional<int64_t> getImm16Operand(Register R,
+                                              MachineRegisterInfo &MRI) {
+  // Shared with the EQ matcher (CmpNZImm16_match): direct G_CONSTANT or a byte-split
+  // G_MERGE_VALUES(i8 lo, i8 hi). See getI16Const.
+  return getI16Const(R, MRI);
+}
+
+// #321 Increment 1d-retry: select a native s16 ALU op `%dst = G_OP %a, %b` whose
+// value lives in an Imag16 zero-page pair (locals, multi-use intermediates) —
+// handles all s16 ALU shapes via zero-page operands:
+//
+//   add:        clc ; lda <a:zp> ; adc <b:zp> ; sta <dst:zp>
+//   sub:        sec ; lda <a:zp> ; sbc <b:zp> ; sta <dst:zp>   (a minuend)
+//   and/or/xor:       lda <a:zp> ; and|ora|eor <b:zp> ; sta <dst:zp>
+//
+// all MLow=1 -> one rep/sep bracket; the carry-init (clc/sec) is M-width-agnostic
+// (MOSInsertREPSEP) so it sits outside the 16-bit run. The accumulator A16 (Ac16
+// vregs Lo/Res) is entered only by LDAImag16 and left only by STAImag16 (which
+// DEFS the dst Imag16) — there is NO COPY between Ac16 and an 8-bit reg, so the
+// register coalescer cannot merge an 8-bit value into A16 (the exact crash that
+// sank the first 1d prototype).
+//
+// Immediate fold: if an operand is a compile-time constant, the ALU op uses the
+// *Imm16 form (`adc #$0345`) instead of materializing the constant into an Imag16
+// pair — for the commutative ops (add/and/or/xor) either operand may be the
+// constant (swap so the register is loaded). SUB has no SBCImm16 (and `x - C` is
+// canonicalized to `x + (-C)` upstream), so it never folds an immediate. The now-
+// dead constant def is removed automatically by isTriviallyDead in the generic
+// InstructionSelect pass.
+//
+// Mixed-operand fold: a single near-abs global operand (the other being an Imag16
+// register — a local/multi-use value, the case the both-global combiner peephole
+// can't reach) is read directly instead of being materialized into an Imag16 pair.
+// Two independent fold sites: operand A folds into the LHS `lda abs` (LDAbs16);
+// operand B folds into the absolute ALU form (`adc|sbc|and|ora|eor abs`). SUB stays
+// correct without a swap — the minuend is always A (loaded), the subtrahend B. Same
+// 1-to-1 volatile-safe single-use fold as the compare-operand fold (selectSbc16).
+bool MOSInstructionSelector::selectAlu16Native(MachineInstr &MI) {
+  MachineIRBuilder Builder(MI);
+  MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = *Builder.getMRI();
+
+  Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  Register A = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+  Register B = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+
+  // Map the generic opcode to (zp ALU opcode, abs ALU opcode, imm ALU opcode
+  // [0 = none], carry-init: 0 clc / -1 sec (the sign-extended i1 'true' the .td
+  // LDImm1 pattern already produces elsewhere, matching the baseline ADD/SUB
+  // CarryInVal convention below — irrelevant/unused for the non-arith ops),
+  // is-arithmetic, commutative). AbsOpc reads a near-abs global operand
+  // directly (the mixed-operand fold below).
+  unsigned AluOpc, AbsOpc, ImmOpc;
+  int CarryInit;
+  bool IsArith, Commutative;
+  switch (MI.getOpcode()) {
+  case MOS::G_ADD: AluOpc = MOS::ADCImag16; AbsOpc = MOS::ADCAbs16; ImmOpc = MOS::ADCImm16; CarryInit = 0;  IsArith = true;  Commutative = true;  break;
+  case MOS::G_SUB: AluOpc = MOS::SBCImag16; AbsOpc = MOS::SBCAbs16; ImmOpc = 0;             CarryInit = -1; IsArith = true;  Commutative = false; break;
+  case MOS::G_AND: AluOpc = MOS::ANDImag16; AbsOpc = MOS::ANDAbs16; ImmOpc = MOS::ANDImm16; CarryInit = -1; IsArith = false; Commutative = true;  break;
+  case MOS::G_OR:  AluOpc = MOS::ORAImag16; AbsOpc = MOS::ORAAbs16; ImmOpc = MOS::ORAImm16; CarryInit = -1; IsArith = false; Commutative = true;  break;
+  default:         AluOpc = MOS::EORImag16; AbsOpc = MOS::EORAbs16; ImmOpc = MOS::EORImm16; CarryInit = -1; IsArith = false; Commutative = true;  break; // G_XOR
+  }
+
+  // #321 native 16-bit inc/dec: a 16-bit ±1 is one `inc a`/`dec a` on A16 (1 byte, no
+  // carry-init), not `(clc;) adc #$0001`. Compute the signed step from a constant
+  // operand: ADD by +1 / SUB by -1 -> inc; ADD by -1 (0xFFFF) / SUB by +1 -> dec. For
+  // ADD (commutative) the constant may be either operand; for SUB it must be the
+  // subtrahend B (`1 - x` is not a decrement). The value loads via the LHS abs-fold
+  // (lda abs near-abs global, else lda zp); inc/dec a sets N/Z (carry-out unused). All
+  // MLow=1 -> one rep/sep bracket, value enters/leaves A16 only via the load/store.
+  if (MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_ADD || MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_SUB) {
+    auto stepOf = [](int64_t C) -> int { return C == 1 ? 1 : (C == 0xFFFF ? -1 : 0); };
+    int Step = 0;
+    Register Val;
+    if (MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_ADD) {
+      if (auto C = getImm16Operand(B, MRI)) { Step = stepOf(*C); Val = A; }
+      if (!Step)
+        if (auto C = getImm16Operand(A, MRI)) { Step = stepOf(*C); Val = B; }
+    } else { // G_SUB: only the subtrahend (B) as a constant is an inc/dec
+      if (auto C = getImm16Operand(B, MRI)) { Step = -stepOf(*C); Val = A; }
+    }
+    if (Step) {
+      SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 3> New;
+      MachineInstr *LdV = foldableAbsLoad16(Val, MI, MRI, AA);
+      Register Lo = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+      if (LdV)
+        New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAbs16).addDef(Lo)
+                          .add(LdV->getOperand(1)).cloneMemRefs(*LdV));
+      else
+        New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAImag16).addDef(Lo).addUse(Val));
+      Register Res = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+      New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(Step > 0 ? MOS::INCAcc16 : MOS::DECAcc16)
+                        .addDef(Res).addUse(Lo));
+      New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAImag16).addDef(Dst).addUse(Res));
+      for (MachineInstrBuilder &Bld : New)
+        constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+      if (LdV)
+        LdV->eraseFromParent();
+      MI.eraseFromParent();
+      return true;
+    }
+  }
+
+  // Fold a constant operand into the immediate form (not for SUB: no SBCImm16).
+  // After the (optional) swap, A is the register loaded into A16 and Imm (if set)
+  // is the second operand; otherwise B is the register second operand.
+  std::optional<int64_t> Imm;
+  if (ImmOpc) {
+    if ((Imm = getImm16Operand(B, MRI))) {
+      // B is the immediate; A stays the loaded register.
+    } else if (Commutative && (Imm = getImm16Operand(A, MRI))) {
+      std::swap(A, B); // load the old-B register; the old-A constant is the imm.
+    }
+  }
+
+  // Mixed-operand fold sites: a single-use near-abs global A folds into `lda abs`;
+  // a single-use near-abs global B (no immediate) folds into the `OP abs` form.
+  SmallVector<MachineInstr *, 2> Folded;
+  MachineInstr *LdA = foldableAbsLoad16(A, MI, MRI, AA);
+  MachineInstr *LdB = Imm ? nullptr : foldableAbsLoad16(B, MI, MRI, AA);
+
+  SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 4> New;
+
+  // lda a (16-bit) -> A16. Fold a near-abs global LHS straight into `lda abs`.
+  Register Lo = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+  if (LdA) {
+    New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAbs16)
+                      .addDef(Lo)
+                      .add(LdA->getOperand(1))
+                      .cloneMemRefs(*LdA));
+    Folded.push_back(LdA);
+  } else {
+    New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAImag16).addDef(Lo).addUse(A));
+  }
+
+  Register Carry;
+  if (IsArith) {
+    // clc (add) / sec (sub) — outside the 16-bit run (carry-init is M-agnostic).
+    Carry = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::CcRegClass);
+    New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDImm1).addDef(Carry).addImm(CarryInit));
+  }
+
+  // the ALU op (16-bit): A16 = a OP (b | [b:abs] | #imm) (+carry for add/sub). The
+  // second operand is an immediate, a near-abs global (abs form), or an Imag16 reg.
+  Register Res = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+  unsigned EffAluOpc = Imm ? ImmOpc : (LdB ? AbsOpc : AluOpc);
+  auto Alu = Builder.buildInstr(EffAluOpc).addDef(Res);
+  if (IsArith) {
+    Register CarryOut = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::CcRegClass);
+    Register VOut = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::VcRegClass);
+    Alu.addDef(CarryOut).addDef(VOut).addUse(Lo);
+    if (Imm)
+      Alu.addImm(*Imm);
+    else if (LdB)
+      Alu.add(LdB->getOperand(1));
+    else
+      Alu.addUse(B);
+    Alu.addUse(Carry);
+  } else {
+    Alu.addUse(Lo);
+    if (Imm)
+      Alu.addImm(*Imm);
+    else if (LdB)
+      Alu.add(LdB->getOperand(1));
+    else
+      Alu.addUse(B);
+  }
+  if (LdB) {
+    Alu.cloneMemRefs(*LdB);
+    Folded.push_back(LdB);
+  }
+  New.push_back(Alu);
+
+  // sta dst (16-bit): the Imag16 dst is DEF'd by the store (imaginary-reg write).
+  New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAImag16).addDef(Dst).addUse(Res));
+
+  for (MachineInstrBuilder &Bld : New)
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+
+  // Erase the folded loads (single-use; this op was their only consumer, and
+  // selection is bottom-up so they are not yet selected).
+  for (MachineInstr *F : Folded)
+    F->eraseFromParent();
+
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+// #321 xy16: select G_* ops that use the 16-bit X/Y index registers (Xc16/Yc16),
+// routed here before selectImpl by the early `STI.hasIndex16() && selectXY16(MI)` gate.
+// Returns false for ops it doesn't own (the default case) → they fall through to selectImpl.
+//
+// Handles:
+//  C1 — direct register ops: G_LOAD/STORE16_ABS/INDIR + dst/val∈Xc16/Yc16 → LDX/STX/LDY/STY
+//       G_ADD/SUB ±1 → INX16/DEX16/INY16/DEY16; G_SBC compare → CPX/CPY*16
+//  C2 — indexed addressing: G_LOAD/STORE_ABS/INDIR_IDX16 → LDAbsXIdx16/STAbsXIdx16 etc.
+bool MOSInstructionSelector::selectXY16(MachineInstr &MI) {
+  MachineIRBuilder Builder(MI);
+  MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = *Builder.getMRI();
+
+  switch (MI.getOpcode()) {
+  default:
+    return false;
+
+  // ── C1: direct load/store ────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+  case MOS::G_LOAD16_ABS: {
+    Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    bool IsX = isXc16Reg(Dst, MRI), IsY = isYc16Reg(Dst, MRI);
+    if (!IsX && !IsY)
+      return false;
+    // #321 seed 247/445: a 16-bit value classed Xc16/Yc16 whose only consumers are
+    // COPYs (i.e. it is immediately spilled to Imag16, never used as a real index) is
+    // NOT a genuine index. Loading it straight into X16/Y16 leaves a 16-bit value live
+    // in an index register across unrelated ops — and an intervening 8-bit-index op's
+    // narrowing `sep #$10` zeroes the X/Y high byte, silently corrupting it (the
+    // miscompile). Route such loads through the accumulator instead (reclass the dst to
+    // Imag16 and fall through to selectMem16Abs's LDAbs16→STAImag16). Keep the direct
+    // X16/Y16 load only when the value is genuinely consumed as an index. Selection is
+    // bottom-up, so Dst's users are already selected here.
+    {
+      bool GenuineIndex = false;
+      for (const MachineInstr &U : MRI.use_nodbg_instructions(Dst))
+        if (!U.isCopy()) {
+          GenuineIndex = true;
+          break;
+        }
+      if (!GenuineIndex) {
+        MRI.setRegClass(Dst, &MOS::Imag16RegClass);
+        return false;
+      }
+    }
+    auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(IsX ? MOS::LDXAbs16 : MOS::LDYAbs16)
+                  .addDef(Dst)
+                  .add(MI.getOperand(1));
+    Ld.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Ld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  case MOS::G_STORE16_ABS: {
+    Register Val = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    bool IsX = isXc16Reg(Val, MRI), IsY = isYc16Reg(Val, MRI);
+    if (!IsX && !IsY)
+      return false;
+    auto St = Builder.buildInstr(IsX ? MOS::STXAbs16 : MOS::STYAbs16)
+                  .addUse(Val)
+                  .add(MI.getOperand(1));
+    St.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*St.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  case MOS::G_LOAD16_INDIR: {
+    // Load s16 via ZP-resident pointer into X16 or Y16.
+    // A16 = lda (zp); ZP_tmp = sta zp; X/Y = ldx/ldy zp_tmp.
+    Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    bool IsX = isXc16Reg(Dst, MRI), IsY = isYc16Reg(Dst, MRI);
+    if (!IsX && !IsY)
+      return false;
+    Register BasePtr = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+    Register TmpA16 = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+    Register TmpZP = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Imag16RegClass);
+    auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAIndir16).addDef(TmpA16).addUse(BasePtr);
+    Ld.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    auto StZP = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAImag16).addDef(TmpZP).addUse(TmpA16);
+    auto LdXY = Builder.buildInstr(IsX ? MOS::LDXImag16 : MOS::LDYImag16)
+                    .addDef(Dst)
+                    .addUse(TmpZP);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Ld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*StZP.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*LdXY.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  case MOS::G_STORE16_INDIR: {
+    // Store X16 or Y16 via ZP-resident pointer.
+    // stx/sty zp_tmp; A16 = lda zp_tmp; sta (zp).
+    Register Val = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    bool IsX = isXc16Reg(Val, MRI), IsY = isYc16Reg(Val, MRI);
+    if (!IsX && !IsY)
+      return false;
+    Register BasePtr = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+    Register TmpZP = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Imag16RegClass);
+    Register TmpA16 = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+    auto StXY = Builder.buildInstr(IsX ? MOS::STXImag16 : MOS::STYImag16)
+                    .addDef(TmpZP)
+                    .addUse(Val);
+    auto LdA = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAImag16).addDef(TmpA16).addUse(TmpZP);
+    auto St = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAIndir16).addUse(TmpA16).addUse(BasePtr);
+    St.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*StXY.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*LdA.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*St.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  // ── C1: ±1 inc/dec ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+  case MOS::G_ADD: {
+    if (MRI.getType(MI.getOperand(0).getReg()) != LLT::scalar(16))
+      return false;
+    Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    // G_ADD is commutative; constant may be on either side.
+    Register A = MI.getOperand(1).getReg(), B = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+    Register Src = 0;
+    std::optional<int64_t> Step;
+    if (auto C = getI16Const(B, MRI)) { Src = A; Step = C; }
+    else if (auto C = getI16Const(A, MRI)) { Src = B; Step = C; }
+    if (!Src || !Step || (*Step != 1 && *Step != 0xFFFF))
+      return false;
+    unsigned Opc;
+    if (isXc16Reg(Src, MRI))
+      Opc = (*Step == 1) ? MOS::INX16 : MOS::DEX16;
+    else if (isYc16Reg(Src, MRI))
+      Opc = (*Step == 1) ? MOS::INY16 : MOS::DEY16;
+    else
+      return false;
+    auto II = Builder.buildInstr(Opc).addDef(Dst).addUse(Src);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*II.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  case MOS::G_SUB: {
+    if (MRI.getType(MI.getOperand(0).getReg()) != LLT::scalar(16))
+      return false;
+    Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    Register Src = MI.getOperand(1).getReg(); // G_SUB is not commutative
+    Register RHS = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+    auto Step = getI16Const(RHS, MRI);
+    if (!Step || (*Step != 1 && *Step != 0xFFFF))
+      return false;
+    unsigned Opc;
+    if (isXc16Reg(Src, MRI))
+      Opc = (*Step == 1) ? MOS::DEX16 : MOS::INX16; // sub 1 → dec; sub -1 → inc
+    else if (isYc16Reg(Src, MRI))
+      Opc = (*Step == 1) ? MOS::DEY16 : MOS::INY16;
+    else
+      return false;
+    auto II = Builder.buildInstr(Opc).addDef(Dst).addUse(Src);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*II.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  // ── C1: compare (G_SBC in compare form, LHS∈Xc16 or Yc16) ───────────────
+
+  case MOS::G_SBC: {
+    if (MRI.getType(MI.getOperand(5).getReg()) != LLT::scalar(16))
+      return false;
+    Register LHS = MI.getOperand(5).getReg();
+    Register CarryOut = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+    Register RHS = MI.getOperand(6).getReg();
+    bool IsX = isXc16Reg(LHS, MRI), IsY = !IsX && isYc16Reg(LHS, MRI);
+    if (!IsX && !IsY)
+      return false;
+    unsigned CPAbs  = IsX ? MOS::CPXAbs16  : MOS::CPYAbs16;
+    unsigned CPImag = IsX ? MOS::CPXImag16 : MOS::CPYImag16;
+    unsigned CPImm  = IsX ? MOS::CPXImm16  : MOS::CPYImm16;
+    MachineInstrBuilder CmpI;
+    if (auto Imm = getImm16Operand(RHS, MRI)) {
+      CmpI = Builder.buildInstr(CPImm).addDef(CarryOut).addUse(LHS).addImm(*Imm);
+    } else if (MachineInstr *LdR = foldableAbsLoad16(RHS, MI, MRI, AA)) {
+      CmpI = Builder.buildInstr(CPAbs)
+                 .addDef(CarryOut)
+                 .addUse(LHS)
+                 .add(LdR->getOperand(1))
+                 .cloneMemRefs(*LdR);
+      LdR->eraseFromParent();
+    } else {
+      CmpI = Builder.buildInstr(CPImag).addDef(CarryOut).addUse(LHS).addUse(RHS);
+    }
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*CmpI.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  // ── C2: indexed addressing (16-bit X/Y as array index) ───────────────────
+
+  case MOS::G_LOAD_ABS_IDX16: {
+    Register ValOrDst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    const MachineOperand &BaseOp = MI.getOperand(1);
+    Register IdxReg = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+    LLT ValTy = MRI.getType(ValOrDst);
+    SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 3> New;
+    // If the index is in Imag16 (or is an unclassed s16 vreg that will land in
+    // Imag16), move it into X16 first. getRegClassOrNull returns null for vregs
+    // not yet constrained by selection (GIS selects bottom-up); those default to
+    // Imag16 later, so we must handle them here too.
+    Register XIdx = IdxReg;
+    {
+      const TargetRegisterClass *RC = MRI.getRegClassOrNull(IdxReg);
+      if ((RC && RC->hasSuperClassEq(&MOS::Imag16RegClass)) ||
+          (!RC && MRI.getType(IdxReg) == LLT::scalar(16))) {
+        XIdx = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Xc16RegClass);
+        New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDXImag16).addDef(XIdx).addUse(IdxReg));
+      }
+    }
+    if (ValTy == LLT::scalar(16)) {
+      // 16-bit data, 16-bit X: rep #$30; lda abs,X; sep #$30 → A16 → Imag16
+      Register A16 = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+      auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAbsXIdx16).addDef(A16).add(BaseOp).addUse(XIdx);
+      Ld.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+      New.push_back(Ld);
+      New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAImag16).addDef(ValOrDst).addUse(A16));
+    } else {
+      // 8-bit data, 16-bit X: rep #$10; lda abs,X; sep #$10 → Ac
+      auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAbsXIdx).addDef(ValOrDst).add(BaseOp).addUse(XIdx);
+      Ld.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+      New.push_back(Ld);
+    }
+    for (auto &Bld : New)
+      constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  case MOS::G_STORE_ABS_IDX16: {
+    Register ValOrDst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    const MachineOperand &BaseOp = MI.getOperand(1);
+    Register IdxReg = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+    LLT ValTy = MRI.getType(ValOrDst);
+    SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 3> New;
+    MachineInstr *FoldedLoad = nullptr;
+    Register XIdx = IdxReg;
+    {
+      const TargetRegisterClass *RC = MRI.getRegClassOrNull(IdxReg);
+      if ((RC && RC->hasSuperClassEq(&MOS::Imag16RegClass)) ||
+          (!RC && MRI.getType(IdxReg) == LLT::scalar(16))) {
+        XIdx = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Xc16RegClass);
+        New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDXImag16).addDef(XIdx).addUse(IdxReg));
+      }
+    }
+    if (ValTy == LLT::scalar(16)) {
+      // 16-bit data, 16-bit X: Imag16 → A16; rep #$30; sta abs,X; sep #$30
+      Register A16 = loadStoreValueIntoA16(Builder, MI, ValOrDst, New, FoldedLoad);
+      auto St = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAbsXIdx16).addUse(A16).add(BaseOp).addUse(XIdx);
+      St.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+      New.push_back(St);
+    } else {
+      // 8-bit data, 16-bit X: rep #$10; sta abs,X; sep #$10
+      auto St = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAbsXIdx).addUse(ValOrDst).add(BaseOp).addUse(XIdx);
+      St.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+      New.push_back(St);
+    }
+    for (auto &Bld : New)
+      constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    if (FoldedLoad)
+      FoldedLoad->eraseFromParent();
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  case MOS::G_LOAD_INDIR_IDX16: {
+    Register ValOrDst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    Register BasePtr = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+    Register IdxReg = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+    LLT ValTy = MRI.getType(ValOrDst);
+    SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 3> New;
+    // If the index is in Imag16 (or is an unclassed s16 vreg), move it into Y16.
+    Register YIdx = IdxReg;
+    {
+      const TargetRegisterClass *RC = MRI.getRegClassOrNull(IdxReg);
+      if ((RC && RC->hasSuperClassEq(&MOS::Imag16RegClass)) ||
+          (!RC && MRI.getType(IdxReg) == LLT::scalar(16))) {
+        YIdx = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Yc16RegClass);
+        New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDYImag16).addDef(YIdx).addUse(IdxReg));
+      }
+    }
+    if (ValTy == LLT::scalar(16)) {
+      // 16-bit data, 16-bit Y: rep #$30; lda (zp),Y; sep #$30 → A16 → Imag16
+      Register A16 = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+      auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDIndirYIdx16).addDef(A16).addUse(BasePtr).addUse(YIdx);
+      Ld.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+      New.push_back(Ld);
+      New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAImag16).addDef(ValOrDst).addUse(A16));
+    } else {
+      // 8-bit data, 16-bit Y: rep #$10; lda (zp),Y; sep #$10 → Ac
+      auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDIndirYIdx).addDef(ValOrDst).addUse(BasePtr).addUse(YIdx);
+      Ld.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+      New.push_back(Ld);
+    }
+    for (auto &Bld : New)
+      constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  // #321 Phase 2 inc 2: `lda [dp],y` / `sta [dp],y` with a 16-bit Y
+  // (+mos-xy16), 8-bit data. The index stays in an Imag16 pair: the FUSED
+  // pseudo loads it into Y itself (see LDIndirLongYIdx in MOSInstrLogical.td
+  // for why a separate LDYImag16 -> Yc16 is unsafe here). The far pointer is
+  // pinned to Imag32 exactly as selectGeneric pins it for the 8-bit-Y forms
+  // (otherwise a class-less bridge COPY is left behind).
+  // docs/plans/2026-09-25-dpy-indexed-phase2-increment2.md §3.3.
+  case MOS::G_LOAD_FAR_INDIR_IDX16:
+  case MOS::G_STORE_FAR_INDIR_IDX16: {
+    const bool IsLoad = MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD_FAR_INDIR_IDX16;
+    Register ValOrDst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    Register FarPtr = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+    Register IdxReg = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+    assert(MRI.getType(ValOrDst) == LLT::scalar(8) &&
+           "far indirect-long indexed access is 8-bit data only");
+    assert(MRI.getType(IdxReg) == LLT::scalar(16) && "16-bit Y index");
+    if (FarPtr.isVirtual() && !MRI.getRegClassOrNull(FarPtr))
+      MRI.setRegClass(FarPtr, &MOS::Imag32RegClass);
+    auto Mem = IsLoad ? Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDIndirLongYIdx)
+                            .addDef(ValOrDst)
+                            .addUse(FarPtr)
+                            .addUse(IdxReg)
+                      : Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STIndirLongYIdx)
+                            .addUse(ValOrDst)
+                            .addUse(FarPtr)
+                            .addUse(IdxReg);
+    Mem.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Mem, TII, TRI, RBI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  case MOS::G_STORE_INDIR_IDX16: {
+    Register ValOrDst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+    Register BasePtr = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+    Register IdxReg = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+    LLT ValTy = MRI.getType(ValOrDst);
+    SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 3> New;
+    MachineInstr *FoldedLoad = nullptr;
+    Register YIdx = IdxReg;
+    {
+      const TargetRegisterClass *RC = MRI.getRegClassOrNull(IdxReg);
+      if ((RC && RC->hasSuperClassEq(&MOS::Imag16RegClass)) ||
+          (!RC && MRI.getType(IdxReg) == LLT::scalar(16))) {
+        YIdx = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Yc16RegClass);
+        New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDYImag16).addDef(YIdx).addUse(IdxReg));
+      }
+    }
+    if (ValTy == LLT::scalar(16)) {
+      // 16-bit data, 16-bit Y: Imag16 → A16; rep #$30; sta (zp),Y; sep #$30
+      Register A16 = loadStoreValueIntoA16(Builder, MI, ValOrDst, New, FoldedLoad);
+      auto St = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STIndirYIdx16).addUse(A16).addUse(BasePtr).addUse(YIdx);
+      St.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+      New.push_back(St);
+    } else {
+      // 8-bit data, 16-bit Y: rep #$10; sta (zp),Y; sep #$10
+      auto St = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STIndirYIdx).addUse(ValOrDst).addUse(BasePtr).addUse(YIdx);
+      St.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+      New.push_back(St);
+    }
+    for (auto &Bld : New)
+      constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+    if (FoldedLoad)
+      FoldedLoad->eraseFromParent();
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+  }
+}
+
+// #321 native s16 constant shift: `dst = src << k` / `dst = src >> k` (unsigned),
+// k a compile-time constant in [1,7] left un-narrowed by the legalizer under
+// +mos-a16. In 16-bit-accumulator mode one `asl a` / `lsr a` shifts all 16 bits,
+// so emit `lda src; (asl|lsr)*k; sta dst` — all MLow=1, so MOSInsertREPSEP wraps
+// the run in one rep/sep. The value enters A16 only via LDAImag16 and leaves only
+// via STAImag16 (no Ac16<->8-bit COPY). No carry-init: each shift self-fills 0.
+bool MOSInstructionSelector::selectShift16Native(MachineInstr &MI) {
+  MachineIRBuilder Builder(MI);
+  MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = *Builder.getMRI();
+
+  Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  Register Src = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+  Register AmtReg = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+
+  auto AmtVal = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(AmtReg, MRI);
+  if (!AmtVal)
+    return false; // legalizer only leaves constant amounts native; be defensive
+  uint64_t Amt = AmtVal->Value.getZExtValue();
+
+  const unsigned Opc = MI.getOpcode();
+  const bool IsAshr = Opc == MOS::G_ASHR;
+
+  SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 16> New;
+
+  // lda src (16-bit) -> A16
+  Register Cur = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+  New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAImag16).addDef(Cur).addUse(Src));
+
+  // Amt iterations on A16, each threading a fresh Ac16 from the previous (the tied
+  // $dst=$src collapses the chain onto A16 in regalloc).
+  //  - G_SHL  -> asl a  (fill 0 at bit 0)
+  //  - G_LSHR -> lsr a  (fill 0 at bit 15)
+  //  - G_ASHR -> cmp #$8000; ror a  (cmp sets C = sign bit; ror rotates it into
+  //              bit 15, replicating the sign; the ror's carry-out is dead).
+  for (uint64_t I = 0; I < Amt; ++I) {
+    Register Next = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+    if (IsAshr) {
+      Register Sign = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::CcRegClass);
+      New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::CMPImm16)
+                        .addDef(Sign)
+                        .addUse(Cur)
+                        .addImm(0x8000));
+      Register CarryOut = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::CcRegClass);
+      New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::RORAcc16)
+                        .addDef(Next)
+                        .addDef(CarryOut)
+                        .addUse(Cur)
+                        .addUse(Sign));
+    } else {
+      const unsigned ShOpc = Opc == MOS::G_SHL ? MOS::ASLAcc16 : MOS::LSRAcc16;
+      New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(ShOpc).addDef(Next).addUse(Cur));
+    }
+    Cur = Next;
+  }
+
+  // sta dst (16-bit): the Imag16 dst is DEF'd by the store (imaginary-reg write).
+  New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAImag16).addDef(Dst).addUse(Cur));
+
+  for (MachineInstrBuilder &Bld : New)
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+Register MOSInstructionSelector::loadStoreValueIntoA16(
+    MachineIRBuilder &Builder, MachineInstr &StoreMI, Register Value,
+    SmallVectorImpl<MachineInstrBuilder> &New, MachineInstr *&FoldedLoad) {
+  MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = *Builder.getMRI();
+  FoldedLoad = nullptr;
+  Register A16 = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+  MachineInstr *Def = MRI.getVRegDef(Value);
+  if (Def && MRI.hasOneNonDBGUse(Value) &&
+      (Def->getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_ABS ||
+       Def->getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_INDIR ||
+       Def->getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS ||
+       Def->getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_INDIR) &&
+      shouldFoldMemAccess(StoreMI, *Def, AA)) {
+    // Indirect/mixed copy: fold the load directly into A16 (no Imag16 temp). The
+    // load's operand 1 is its address (abs operand or pointer); operand 0 is the
+    // value vreg this store consumes.
+    unsigned LoadOpcode;
+    switch (Def->getOpcode()) {
+    case MOS::G_LOAD16_ABS: LoadOpcode = MOS::LDAbs16; break;
+    case MOS::G_LOAD16_INDIR: LoadOpcode = MOS::LDAIndir16; break;
+    case MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS: LoadOpcode = MOS::LDAbsLong16; break;
+    default: LoadOpcode = MOS::LDIndirLong16; break;
+    }
+    auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(LoadOpcode)
+                  .addDef(A16)
+                  .add(Def->getOperand(1));
+    Ld.cloneMemRefs(*Def);
+    New.push_back(Ld);
+    FoldedLoad = Def;
+  } else {
+    // Value is already a 16-bit register (Imag16): load it into A16.
+    New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::LDAImag16).addDef(A16).addUse(Value));
+  }
+  return A16;
+}
+
+// #321 native s16 indirect load/store: a 16-bit value through a runtime pointer.
+//  G_LOAD16_INDIR  (dst s16, ptr) -> lda (zp)16 -> A16; sta zp16 -> Imag16 dst
+//  G_STORE16_INDIR (val s16, ptr) -> lda zp16 (val -> A16); sta (zp)16
+// The pointer operand is constrained to the Imag16 zero-page pair (same as the
+// 8-bit LDIndir/STIndir); the value enters/leaves A16 only via the Imag16 load/
+// store -> no Ac16<->8-bit COPY. Both ops are MLow=1, so MOSInsertREPSEP brackets
+// the pair with one rep/sep.
+bool MOSInstructionSelector::selectMem16Indir(MachineInstr &MI) {
+  MachineIRBuilder Builder(MI);
+  MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = *Builder.getMRI();
+  const bool IsLoad = MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_INDIR ||
+                      MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_INDIR;
+  const bool Far = MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_INDIR ||
+                   MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_INDIR;
+
+  Register ValOrDst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  Register Ptr = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+  if (Far && Ptr.isVirtual() && !MRI.getRegClassOrNull(Ptr))
+    MRI.setRegClass(Ptr, &MOS::Imag32RegClass);
+
+  // The user's memory access is the indirect op (LDAIndir16 / STAIndir16); carry
+  // the memref there. The Imag16 load/store that moves the value to/from A16 is an
+  // internal zp register transfer with no memref (same pattern as foldableAbsLoad16).
+  SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 2> New;
+  MachineInstr *FoldedLoad = nullptr;
+  if (IsLoad) {
+    Register A16 = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+    auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(Far ? MOS::LDIndirLong16 : MOS::LDAIndir16)
+                  .addDef(A16).addUse(Ptr);
+    Ld.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    New.push_back(Ld);
+    New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAImag16).addDef(ValOrDst).addUse(A16));
+  } else {
+    Register A16 = loadStoreValueIntoA16(Builder, MI, ValOrDst, New, FoldedLoad);
+    auto St = Builder.buildInstr(Far ? MOS::STIndirLong16 : MOS::STAIndir16)
+                  .addUse(A16).addUse(Ptr);
+    St.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    New.push_back(St);
+  }
+
+  for (MachineInstrBuilder &Bld : New)
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+  if (FoldedLoad)
+    FoldedLoad->eraseFromParent();
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+// #321 native s16 absolute load/store: a 16-bit value at an absolute (global/static)
+// address. Mirror of selectMem16Indir, using LDAbs16/STAbs16 (the abs operand rides
+// the real memory op; the Imag16 transfer carries no memref). One rep/sep each; no
+// Ac16<->8-bit COPY.
+//  G_LOAD16_ABS  (dst s16, absOp) -> lda abs16 -> A16; sta zp16 -> Imag16 dst
+//  G_STORE16_ABS (val s16, absOp) -> lda zp16 (val -> A16); sta abs16
+bool MOSInstructionSelector::selectMem16Abs(MachineInstr &MI) {
+  MachineIRBuilder Builder(MI);
+  MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = *Builder.getMRI();
+  const bool IsLoad = MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_ABS ||
+                      MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS;
+  const bool Far = MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS ||
+                   MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_ABS;
+
+  Register ValOrDst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  const MachineOperand &AbsOp = MI.getOperand(1);
+
+  SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 2> New;
+  MachineInstr *FoldedLoad = nullptr;
+  if (IsLoad) {
+    Register A16 = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+    auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(Far ? MOS::LDAbsLong16 : MOS::LDAbs16)
+                  .addDef(A16).add(AbsOp);
+    Ld.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    New.push_back(Ld);
+    New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAImag16).addDef(ValOrDst).addUse(A16));
+  } else {
+    Register A16 = loadStoreValueIntoA16(Builder, MI, ValOrDst, New, FoldedLoad);
+    auto St = Builder.buildInstr(Far ? MOS::STAbsLong16 : MOS::STAbs16)
+                  .addUse(A16).add(AbsOp);
+    St.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    New.push_back(St);
+  }
+
+  for (MachineInstrBuilder &Bld : New)
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+  if (FoldedLoad)
+    FoldedLoad->eraseFromParent();
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+bool MOSInstructionSelector::selectMem16AbsIdx(MachineInstr &MI) {
+  MachineIRBuilder Builder(MI);
+  MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = *Builder.getMRI();
+  const bool IsLoad = MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_ABS_IDX ||
+                      MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS_IDX ||
+                      MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS_IDX16;
+  const bool Far = MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS_IDX ||
+                   MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_ABS_IDX ||
+                   MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS_IDX16 ||
+                   MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_ABS_IDX16;
+  const bool WideIndex = MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS_IDX16 ||
+                         MI.getOpcode() == MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_ABS_IDX16;
+
+  Register ValOrDst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  const MachineOperand &BaseOp = MI.getOperand(1);
+  Register IdxReg = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+
+  SmallVector<MachineInstrBuilder, 2> New;
+  MachineInstr *FoldedLoad = nullptr;
+  if (IsLoad) {
+    Register A16 = MRI.createVirtualRegister(&MOS::Ac16RegClass);
+    auto Ld = Builder.buildInstr(Far ? (WideIndex ? MOS::LDAbsLongXIdx16
+                                                 : MOS::LDAbsLongIdx16)
+                                     : MOS::LDAbsIdx16)
+                  .addDef(A16).add(BaseOp).addUse(IdxReg);
+    Ld.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    New.push_back(Ld);
+    New.push_back(Builder.buildInstr(MOS::STAImag16).addDef(ValOrDst).addUse(A16));
+  } else {
+    Register A16 = loadStoreValueIntoA16(Builder, MI, ValOrDst, New, FoldedLoad);
+    auto St = Builder.buildInstr(Far ? (WideIndex ? MOS::STAbsLongXIdx16
+                                                 : MOS::STAbsLongIdx16)
+                                     : MOS::STAbsIdx16)
+                  .addUse(A16).add(BaseOp).addUse(IdxReg);
+    St.cloneMemRefs(MI);
+    New.push_back(St);
+  }
+
+  for (MachineInstrBuilder &Bld : New)
+    constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Bld.getInstr(), TII, TRI, RBI);
+  if (FoldedLoad)
+    FoldedLoad->eraseFromParent();
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}

+// #321 Increment 1e: 16-bit (zp),y indexed load/store.
+//  G_LOAD16_INDIR_IDX  (dst s16, base_ptr, idx_reg) → LDIndirIdx16(A16, base, idx) + STAImag16(dst, A16)
+//  G_STORE16_INDIR_IDX (val s16, base_ptr, idx_reg) → LDAImag16(A16, val) + STIndirIdx16(A16, base, idx)
+// One rep/sep via MLow=1 on LDIndirIdx16/STIndirIdx16; no Ac16<->8-bit COPY.
+bool MOSInstructionSelector::selectMem16IndirIdx(MachineInstr &MI) {

+// #321 A16-threading: drop the redundant Imag16 round-trip between dependent
+// native-s16 ops. Each native s16 op is self-contained (LDAImag16 -> OP ->
+// STAImag16, value home = Imag16 between ops — the 1d-retry coalescer-safe
+// invariant), so a dependent chain stores each intermediate and reloads it:
+//     $rsN = STAImag16 $a16        ; store the value into its Imag16 home
+//     $a16 = LDAImag16 $rsN        ; reload it — but $a16 still holds the value!
+// The reload is redundant; erase it, and if the home is dead afterward (only the
+// reload read it, with no reader in between) erase the store too — the value then
+// threads through $a16 across the ops (e.g. lda;adc;and;sbc;sta).
+//
+// POST-RA on purpose: RA has already chosen $a16 for both sides, so collapsing the
+// pair cannot reintroduce the 1d coalescer crash (an 8-bit value coalescing into
+// A16). LDAImag16/STAImag16 model no NZ/flag def, so erasing them is flag-safe.
+//
+// Phase 1.5: the store and reload need NOT be adjacent — intervening instructions
+// are allowed as long as none rewrites $a16 (modifiesRegister(A16) also catches
+// 8-bit sub-A writes and xba; calls/side-effect ops bail explicitly) or overwrites
+// the home $rsN. A *read* of $a16 between (e.g. a second `sta gg`) is fine; we just
+// clear stale $a16 kills across the gap so it stays live to the next op. A missed
+// case only forgoes a win, never miscompiles.
+bool MOSLateOptimization::threadAccum16(MachineBasicBlock &MBB) const {
+  const auto *TRI = MBB.getParent()->getSubtarget().getRegisterInfo();
+  bool Changed = false;
+
+  // Stores whose value still provably resides in $a16, awaiting a redundant reload.
+  struct Pend { Register Home; MachineInstr *Sta; bool HomeRead; };
+  SmallVector<Pend, 4> Pending;
+  auto indexOf = [&](Register H) -> int {
+    for (unsigned I = 0, E = Pending.size(); I != E; ++I)
+      if (Pending[I].Home == H)
+        return (int)I;
+    return -1;
+  };
+
+  for (MachineInstr &MI : make_early_inc_range(MBB)) {
+    if (MI.isDebugInstr())
+      continue;
+
+    if (MI.getOpcode() == MOS::LDAImag16) {
+      Register Home = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();           // Imag16 use ($rsN)
+      int Idx = indexOf(Home);
+      if (Idx >= 0 &&
+          MI.getOperand(0).getReg() == Pending[Idx].Sta->getOperand(1).getReg()) {
+        // Redundant reload: $a16 already holds Home's value.
+        MachineInstr *Sta = Pending[Idx].Sta;
+        Register Acc = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();          // $a16
+        bool EraseStore = MI.getOperand(1).isKill() && !Pending[Idx].HomeRead;
+        // $a16 now stays live from the store across the gap to the next op — clear
+        // any stale kills on it so the verifier/later passes see it live throughout.
+        for (auto J = MachineBasicBlock::iterator(Sta),
+                  E = MachineBasicBlock::iterator(MI);
+             J != E; ++J)
+          J->clearRegisterKills(Acc, TRI);
+        MI.eraseFromParent();
+        Changed = true;
+        if (EraseStore) {
+          Sta->eraseFromParent();
+          Pending.erase(Pending.begin() + Idx); // store gone -> stop tracking
+        }
+        // Else keep BOTH the store and the pending entry: the value still resides
+        // in $a16 *and* the home, so a later reload of the same home is also
+        // redundant (this is the multi-reload case, e.g. `sta h; <store h>; lda h;
+        // … ; lda h`). $a16 is unchanged, so other pendings stay valid too.
+        continue;
+      }
+      // Genuine reload: it rewrites $a16, so no resident value survives.
+      Pending.clear();
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (MI.getOpcode() == MOS::STAImag16) {
+      // A store of the current $a16 value. STA reads $a16 (doesn't write it), so
+      // prior pendings keep holding the same resident value.
+      Register Home = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();           // Imag16 def ($rsN)
+      if (int Old = indexOf(Home); Old >= 0)
+        Pending.erase(Pending.begin() + Old);
+      Pending.push_back({Home, &MI, /*HomeRead=*/false});
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    // Any other instruction. Bail the whole resident set if $a16 is rewritten
+    // (incl. 8-bit sub-A / xba via aliasing -> modifiesRegister), clobbered by a
+    // call (regmask), or touched opaquely by inline asm. A volatile *store* only
+    // READS $a16, so it's fine to thread across (the store stays put; only the
+    // non-volatile reload of the Imag16 temp is dropped) — a volatile *load* would
+    // write $a16 and is caught by modifiesRegister above.
+    if (MI.modifiesRegister(MOS::A16, TRI) || MI.isCall() || MI.isInlineAsm()) {
+      Pending.clear();
+      continue;
+    }
+    // $a16 untouched: drop any home this instruction overwrites; note reads (a read
+    // means the store is still needed and may not be erased).
+    for (unsigned I = 0; I != Pending.size();) {
+      if (MI.modifiesRegister(Pending[I].Home, TRI)) {
+        Pending.erase(Pending.begin() + I);
+      } else {
+        if (MI.readsRegister(Pending[I].Home, TRI))
+          Pending[I].HomeRead = true;
+        ++I;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return Changed;
+}
+
 bool MOSLateOptimization::tailJMP(MachineBasicBlock &MBB) const {
   if (MBB.size() < 2)
     return false;
   auto It = std::prev(MBB.end());
-  if (It->getOpcode() != MOS::RTS)
+  const unsigned RetOp = It->getOpcode();
+  // Near return (JSR;RTS) or, on W65816, a far return (JSL;RTL).
+  if (RetOp != MOS::RTS && RetOp != MOS::RTL)
     return false;
-  MachineInstr &RTS = *It;
+  MachineInstr &Ret = *It;
   --It;
-  if (It->getOpcode() != MOS::JSR)
+  const auto &TII = *MBB.getParent()->getSubtarget().getInstrInfo();
+
+  // Near tail call: `JSR g; RTS` -> `TailJMP g` (a near $4C jump; g's own RTS
+  // returns to the original caller).
+  if (RetOp == MOS::RTS) {
+    if (It->getOpcode() != MOS::JSR)
+      return false;
+    Ret.eraseFromParent();
+    It->setDesc(TII.get(MOS::TailJMP));
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  // #320 far tail call: a far block tail `JSL <far target>; RTL` -> `TailJML` (a
+  // direct 24-bit long jump $5C; the far target's own RTL pops the caller's 3-byte
+  // JSL return, landing at the original caller — tail-call style). The RTL
+  // terminator already proves the current function is far (lowerReturn emits RTL
+  // only for a .far_ frame), so the dangerous near->far shape `JSL g; RTS` can
+  // never reach here. Three direct, provably-far callees fold — each matched
+  // precisely so any other JSL is left alone (conservative: a misclassification
+  // can only miss a win, never emit a wrong-bank jump or a mismatched return):
+  //   (a) a direct far-function GLOBAL (isGlobal + a .far_ section);
+  //   (b) the far->near mixed-banking thunk __call_near_from_far, which lowerCall
+  //       ChangeToES's to an EXTERNAL symbol (so isGlobal() is false — match it by
+  //       name, mirroring how MOSCallLowering special-cases the same symbol). Its
+  //       body `pea .Lback-1; jmp (__rc18); .Lback: rtl` keeps a net-0 near frame
+  //       above our 3-byte return, so its terminal RTL pops the ORIGINAL caller's
+  //       return — the tail contract holds (the folded JSL's own 3-byte return is
+  //       exactly what the long jump elides); and
+  //   (c) the far-indirect thunk __call_indir_far (a bank-0 GLOBAL in a non-.far_
+  //       section — match it by name). Its body `jml (__mos_far_target)` pushes
+  //       NOTHING and never returns to the thunk, so the far target's own RTL pops
+  //       the ORIGINAL caller's 3-byte return — the same tail contract, proof even
+  //       simpler than (b) since the stack is untouched between the long jump and
+  //       the far target. (The slot store + JSL precede this block; the tail is
+  //       exactly `JSL __call_indir_far; RTL`.)
+  if (It->getOpcode() != MOS::JSL)
+    return false;
+  const MachineOperand &Tgt = It->getOperand(0);
+  const bool DirectFarGlobal =
+      Tgt.isGlobal() && Tgt.getGlobal()->getSection().starts_with(".far_");
+  const bool NearFromFarThunk =
+      Tgt.isSymbol() &&
+      StringRef(Tgt.getSymbolName()) == "__call_near_from_far";
+  const bool IndirFarThunk =
+      Tgt.isGlobal() && Tgt.getGlobal()->getName() == "__call_indir_far";
+  if (!DirectFarGlobal && !NearFromFarThunk && !IndirFarThunk)
     return false;
-  MachineInstr &JSR = *It;
-  RTS.eraseFromParent();
-  JSR.setDesc(JSR.getMF()->getSubtarget().getInstrInfo()->get(MOS::TailJMP));
+  Ret.eraseFromParent();
+  It->setDesc(TII.get(MOS::TailJML));
   return true;
 }

+// #321 a16: a direct 4x s8 -> s32 G_MERGE_VALUES (only reached via customFor({S32,S8})
+// under +mos-a16) — rewrite into the legal 2-level form so it routes through the
+// s32-as-2x-s16 representation: merge(a,b)->s16, merge(c,d)->s16, then those ->s32.
+// Both halves are legal (legalForCartesianProduct {S16,S8}; legalFor {S32,S16}) and the
+// artifact combiner folds them against feeding unmerges. selectMergeValues only takes a
+// 2-source merge, so the 4-source form must be split here, not selected.
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::legalizeMergeS32FromBytes(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                                 MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                                 MachineInstr &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  const LLT S16 = LLT::scalar(16);
+  Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  Register B0 = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+  Register B1 = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+  Register B2 = MI.getOperand(3).getReg();
+  Register B3 = MI.getOperand(4).getReg();
+  auto Lo = Builder.buildMergeValues(S16, {B0, B1});
+  auto Hi = Builder.buildMergeValues(S16, {B2, B3});
+  Builder.buildMergeValues(Dst, {Lo.getReg(0), Hi.getReg(0)});
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+// #321 a16: a direct 4x s8 <- s32 G_UNMERGE_VALUES (only reached via customFor({S8,S32})
+// under +mos-a16) — the symmetric inverse of legalizeMergeS32FromBytes. Rewrite it into
+// the legal 2-level form so it routes through the s32-as-2x-s16 representation: unmerge
+// the s32 into two s16 words (legal {S16,S32}), then unmerge each word into two s8 bytes
+// (legal {S8,S16}), reusing the four original byte def regs. selectUnmergeValues only
+// takes a 2-dest unmerge, so the 4-dest form must be split here, not selected. Building
+// only unmerges (no new merge) cannot create a merge<->unmerge artifact-combine loop.
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::legalizeUnmergeS32ToBytes(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                                 MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                                 MachineInstr &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  const LLT S16 = LLT::scalar(16);
+  Register D0 = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  Register D1 = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+  Register D2 = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+  Register D3 = MI.getOperand(3).getReg();
+  Register Src = MI.getOperand(4).getReg(); // G_UNMERGE: defs first, source last
+  auto Words = Builder.buildUnmerge(S16, Src);     // s32 -> Lo:s16, Hi:s16
+  Builder.buildUnmerge({D0, D1}, Words.getReg(0)); // Lo -> D0, D1
+  Builder.buildUnmerge({D2, D3}, Words.getReg(1)); // Hi -> D2, D3
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+// #321 a16: a direct 4x s16 -> s64 G_MERGE_VALUES (only reached via customFor({S64,S16})
+// under +mos-a16) — the s64 analogue of legalizeMergeS32FromBytes, one level up: merge each
+// pair of s16 words into an s32 (legal {S32,S16}), then merge the two s32 halves into the s64
+// (legal {S64,S32}). selectMergeValues only takes a 2-source merge, so the 4-source form must be
+// split here, not selected.
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::legalizeMergeS64FromWords(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                                 MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                                 MachineInstr &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  const LLT S32 = LLT::scalar(32);
+  Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  Register W0 = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+  Register W1 = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+  Register W2 = MI.getOperand(3).getReg();
+  Register W3 = MI.getOperand(4).getReg();
+  auto Lo = Builder.buildMergeValues(S32, {W0, W1});
+  auto Hi = Builder.buildMergeValues(S32, {W2, W3});
+  Builder.buildMergeValues(Dst, {Lo.getReg(0), Hi.getReg(0)});
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+// #321 a16: a direct 4x s16 <- s64 G_UNMERGE_VALUES (only reached via customFor({S16,S64})
+// under +mos-a16) — the symmetric inverse of legalizeMergeS64FromWords, and the s64 analogue of
+// legalizeUnmergeS32ToBytes: unmerge the s64 into two s32 halves (legal {S32,S64}), then unmerge
+// each half into two s16 words (legal {S16,S32}), reusing the four original word def regs.
+// selectUnmergeValues only takes a 2-dest unmerge, so the 4-dest form must be split here.
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::legalizeUnmergeS64ToWords(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                                 MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                                 MachineInstr &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  const LLT S32 = LLT::scalar(32);
+  Register D0 = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  Register D1 = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+  Register D2 = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+  Register D3 = MI.getOperand(3).getReg();
+  Register Src = MI.getOperand(4).getReg(); // G_UNMERGE: defs first, source last
+  auto Halves = Builder.buildUnmerge(S32, Src);     // s64 -> Lo:s32, Hi:s32
+  Builder.buildUnmerge({D0, D1}, Halves.getReg(0)); // Lo -> D0, D1
+  Builder.buildUnmerge({D2, D3}, Halves.getReg(1)); // Hi -> D2, D3
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}

+// #321: legalize a far-pointer (PF, p2) G_PHI by converting it to an s32 phi.
+// G_PHI is legal only for {s1,s8,p0,p1}; a far pointer carried across a loop
+// back-edge (a far-pointer induction variable) otherwise aborts the backend
+// ("unable to legalize ... G_PHI (p2)"). We ptrtoint each incoming value at the
+// end of its predecessor block, retype the phi to s32, then inttoptr the result
+// back to p2 after the block's phis — the same bridge legalizePtrAdd / far
+// load+store use. The resulting s32 phi hands off to the standard
+// narrowScalar-of-G_PHI(s32)->bytes path (exercised by any i32 loop counter).
+// Modeled on LegalizerHelper::widenScalar's G_PHI case for the insert points.
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::legalizePhi(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                   MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                   MachineInstr &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+
+  Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  LLT IntTy = LLT::scalar(MRI.getType(Dst).getSizeInBits()); // s32 for p2
+
+  Helper.Observer.changingInstr(MI);
+
+  // ptrtoint each incoming far-pointer value at the end of its predecessor
+  // block (operands come in (value, block) pairs).
+  for (unsigned I = 1, E = MI.getNumOperands(); I < E; I += 2) {
+    MachineBasicBlock &Pred = *MI.getOperand(I + 1).getMBB();
+    Builder.setInsertPt(Pred, Pred.getFirstTerminatorForward());
+    auto Int = Builder.buildPtrToInt(IntTy, MI.getOperand(I).getReg());
+    MI.getOperand(I).setReg(Int.getReg(0));
+  }
+
+  // Retype the phi def to the integer, then inttoptr it back to the far pointer
+  // immediately after the block's phis.
+  Register NewDst = MRI.createGenericVirtualRegister(IntTy);
+  MI.getOperand(0).setReg(NewDst);
+  MachineBasicBlock &MBB = *MI.getParent();
+  Builder.setInsertPt(MBB, MBB.getFirstNonPHI());
+  Builder.buildIntToPtr(Dst, NewDst);
+
+  Helper.Observer.changedInstr(MI);
+  return true;
+}

+// #321 xy16 B1 gate: return true if every use of Vreg (an s16 vreg produced by
+// G_LOAD16_ABS) is compatible with the 16-bit X/Y register class — meaning it can
+// be selected without going through A16/Imag16. Compatible uses:
+//   G_STORE16_ABS       (val operand) → STXAbs16/STYAbs16
+//   G_STORE16_INDIR     (val operand) → STXImag16 + LDAImag16 + STAIndir16
+//   G_ADD/G_SUB ±1      (LHS operand) → INX16/DEX16/INY16/DEY16
+//   G_SBC compare form  (LHS=op5)     → CPX*/CPY*16
+//   G_PHI / COPY        → follow the result vreg (cycle-safe via Visited set)
+// Any other use → false (conservative; misclassification must miss a win only).
+static bool allUsesAreXY16Compatible(Register Vreg, const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                     SmallSet<Register, 8> &Visited) {
+  if (!Visited.insert(Vreg).second)
+    return true; // already-visited (cycle from PHI/COPY chain)
+  for (const MachineInstr &Use : MRI.use_nodbg_instructions(Vreg)) {
+    switch (Use.getOpcode()) {
+    case MOS::G_STORE16_ABS:
+    case MOS::G_STORE16_INDIR:
+      if (Use.getOperand(0).getReg() != Vreg)
+        return false; // must be the stored value, not an address
+      break;
+    case TargetOpcode::G_ADD: {
+      if (MRI.getType(Use.getOperand(0).getReg()) != LLT::scalar(16))
+        return false;
+      // Either operand may be Vreg (G_ADD is commutative); the other must be ±1.
+      Register Other = (Use.getOperand(1).getReg() == Vreg)
+                           ? Use.getOperand(2).getReg()
+                           : Use.getOperand(1).getReg();
+      auto C = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(Other, MRI);
+      if (!C)
+        return false;
+      uint64_t V = C->Value.getZExtValue() & 0xFFFF;
+      if (V != 1 && V != 0xFFFF)
+        return false;
+      break;
+    }
+    case TargetOpcode::G_SUB: {
+      if (MRI.getType(Use.getOperand(0).getReg()) != LLT::scalar(16))
+        return false;
+      if (Use.getOperand(1).getReg() != Vreg)
+        return false; // Vreg must be the minuend (LHS); RHS = step to subtract
+      auto C = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(Use.getOperand(2).getReg(), MRI);
+      if (!C)
+        return false;
+      uint64_t V = C->Value.getZExtValue() & 0xFFFF;
+      if (V != 1 && V != 0xFFFF)
+        return false;
+      break;
+    }
+    case MOS::G_SBC: {
+      // Only compare form: LHS=op5, result (op0) dead, carryIn (op7) = 1.
+      if (MRI.getType(Use.getOperand(5).getReg()) != LLT::scalar(16))
+        return false;
+      if (Use.getOperand(5).getReg() != Vreg)
+        return false; // Vreg must be LHS; RHS stays in Imag16
+      if (!MRI.use_nodbg_empty(Use.getOperand(0).getReg()))
+        return false; // result used → arithmetic SBC, not compare
+      auto CarryIn =
+          getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(Use.getOperand(7).getReg(), MRI);
+      if (!CarryIn || CarryIn->Value.isZero())
+        return false; // carryIn not known 1 → not a compare form
+      break;
+    }
+    case TargetOpcode::G_PHI:
+      if (!allUsesAreXY16Compatible(Use.getOperand(0).getReg(), MRI, Visited))
+        return false;
+      break;
+    case TargetOpcode::COPY:
+      if (!allUsesAreXY16Compatible(Use.getOperand(0).getReg(), MRI, Visited))
+        return false;
+      break;
+    default:
+      return false; // unknown use — conservative
+    }
+  }
+  return true;
+}

+// Native s16 accesses use absolute, indexed, or indirect addressing when their
+// operands can benefit from the 16-bit register path. Other accesses narrow to
+// two byte operations.
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::legalizeLoadStore16(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                           MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                           GLoadStore &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  const MOSSubtarget &STI = Builder.getMF().getSubtarget<MOSSubtarget>();
+  Register Ptr = MI.getPointerReg();
+  const bool IsLoad = isa<GLoad>(MI);
+
+  // A byte-built value used only by absolute stores or byte consumers does not
+  // need an Imag16 pair. Keeping these stores byte-wise lets the register allocator
+  // store each byte directly without an accumulator-width transition. Calls,
+  // inline assembly, and cross-block uses can require the bytes to be spilled;
+  // keep those values on the native path, where a single wide reload is cheaper.
+  auto isByteBuiltStoreValue = [&](Register Value) {
+    const MachineInstr *Def = MRI.getVRegDef(Value);
+    if (!Def || Def->getOpcode() != TargetOpcode::G_MERGE_VALUES ||
+        Def->getNumOperands() != 3 ||
+        MRI.getType(Def->getOperand(1).getReg()) != LLT::scalar(8))
+      return false;
+    return all_of(MRI.use_nodbg_instructions(Value), [&](const MachineInstr &Use) {
+      if (Use.getParent() != Def->getParent())
+        return false;
+      for (auto I = std::next(Def->getIterator()); I != Use.getIterator(); ++I)
+        if (I->isCall() || I->isInlineAsm())
+          return false;
+      switch (Use.getOpcode()) {
+      case TargetOpcode::G_UNMERGE_VALUES:
+        return true;
+      case TargetOpcode::G_ADD:
+      case TargetOpcode::G_SUB: {
+        if (Use.getOperand(1).getReg() != Value)
+          return false;
+        auto isABIByte = [&](Register Byte, MCRegister PhysReg) {
+          const MachineInstr *ByteDef = getDefIgnoringCopies(Byte, MRI);
+          return ByteDef && ByteDef->isCopy() &&
+                 ByteDef->getOperand(1).getReg() == PhysReg;
+        };
+        if (!isABIByte(Def->getOperand(1).getReg(), MOS::A) ||
+            !isABIByte(Def->getOperand(2).getReg(), MOS::X))
+          return false;
+        auto Step = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(
+            Use.getOperand(2).getReg(), MRI);
+        return Step && Step->Value.isOne();
+      }
+      case MOS::G_STORE16_ABS:
+        return none_of(Use.memoperands(), [](const MachineMemOperand *MMO) {
+          return MMO->isAtomic();
+        });
+      case TargetOpcode::G_STORE: {
+        const auto &Store = cast<GStore>(Use);
+        return !Store.isAtomic() &&
+               matchAbsoluteAddressing(MRI, Store.getPointerReg()).has_value();
+      }
+      default:
+        return false;
+      }
+    });
+  };
+
+  // A single plain indirect store can consume an A:X value directly. Imaginary
+  // argument pairs and values shared with other stores can favor one wide load.
+  // Allow only copies and byte splits between the merge and its consumers: other
+  // work can clobber A:X or establish a native-width context with cheaper stores.
+  auto isByteIndirectStore = [&]() {
+    // Atomic stores must remain a single memory operation.
+    if (IsLoad || MI.isAtomic())
+      return false;
+    const MachineInstr *PtrDef = getDefIgnoringCopies(Ptr, MRI);
+    if (!PtrDef || !PtrDef->isCopy() ||
+        !MOS::Imag16RegClass.contains(PtrDef->getOperand(1).getReg()))
+      return false;
+    Register Value = cast<GStore>(MI).getValueReg();
+    const MachineInstr *Def = MRI.getVRegDef(Value);
+    if (!Def || Def->getOpcode() != TargetOpcode::G_MERGE_VALUES ||
+        Def->getNumOperands() != 3)
+      return false;
+    auto isABIByte = [&](Register Byte, MCRegister PhysReg) {
+      const MachineInstr *ByteDef = getDefIgnoringCopies(Byte, MRI);
+      return ByteDef && ByteDef->isCopy() &&
+             ByteDef->getOperand(1).getReg() == PhysReg;
+    };
+    if (!isABIByte(Def->getOperand(1).getReg(), MOS::A) ||
+        !isABIByte(Def->getOperand(2).getReg(), MOS::X))
+      return false;
+    return all_of(MRI.use_nodbg_instructions(Value), [&](const MachineInstr &Use) {
+      if (Use.getParent() != Def->getParent() ||
+          (&Use != &MI && Use.getOpcode() != TargetOpcode::G_UNMERGE_VALUES))
+        return false;
+      for (auto I = std::next(Def->getIterator()); I != Use.getIterator(); ++I)
+        if (&*I != &MI && !I->isCopy() && !I->isDebugInstr() &&
+            I->getOpcode() != TargetOpcode::G_UNMERGE_VALUES)
+          return false;
+      return true;
+    });
+  };
+
+  // Don't take a constant-valued store native: materializing the constant into an
+  // Imag16 pair just to `lda zp; sta` is worse than the byte path — a store of zero
+  // wants the STZ-fusion peephole (`rep; stz; sep`), and a small immediate stays a
+  // cheap pair of 8-bit immediate stores. Only register-valued stores win here.
+  if (!IsLoad && !MI.isAtomic() &&
+      getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(cast<GStore>(MI).getValueReg(), MRI))
+    return Helper.narrowScalar(MI, 0, LLT::scalar(8)) ==
+           LegalizerHelper::Legalized;
+
+  // #321: an s16 LOAD whose result is only ever split into bytes (every use is a
+  // G_UNMERGE — e.g. an s16 global feeding an 8-bit-narrowed compare like
+  // `b = (a == c)`) should keep its byte-wise lowering. Forming the native 16-bit
+  // `lda abs -> A16; sta imag16` here only for the consumer to read the bytes straight
+  // back is a wasteful round-trip (worse than the default byte loads). Narrow it
+  // instead. Indirect loads retain the native form.
+  const bool AllUsesUnmerge =
+      IsLoad && !MI.isAtomic() &&
+      !MRI.use_nodbg_empty(MI.getOperand(0).getReg()) &&
+      all_of(MRI.use_nodbg_instructions(MI.getOperand(0).getReg()),
+             [](const MachineInstr &U) {
+               return U.getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_UNMERGE_VALUES;
+             });
+
+  if (STI.hasAccum16() && MRI.getType(Ptr).getScalarSizeInBits() == 32) {
+    // A byte-built ABI value can be stored as two direct byte writes. Native
+    // producers use one M=0 access, and a load split only into bytes gains no
+    // benefit from occupying the wide accumulator.
+    bool ByteStore = false;
+    if (!IsLoad && !MI.isAtomic()) {
+      Register Value = cast<GStore>(MI).getValueReg();
+      const MachineInstr *Def = getDefIgnoringCopies(Value, MRI);
+      ByteStore = Def && Def->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_MERGE_VALUES;
+    }
+    if (!AllUsesUnmerge && !ByteStore) {
+      if (auto Abs = matchAbsoluteAddressing(MRI, Ptr)) {
+        Builder.buildInstr(IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS
+                                  : MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_ABS)
+            .add(MI.getOperand(0))
+            .add(*Abs)
+            .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+        MI.eraseFromParent();
+        return true;
+      }
+      if (tryFarAbsoluteIndexedAddressing(Helper, MRI, MI) ||
+          tryFarIndirectIndexedAddressing(Helper, MRI, MI))
+        return true;
+      Builder.buildInstr(IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_INDIR
+                                : MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_INDIR)
+          .add(MI.getOperand(0))
+          .addUse(Ptr)
+          .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+      MI.eraseFromParent();
+      return true;
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (STI.hasAccum16() && MRI.getType(Ptr).getScalarSizeInBits() == 16) {
+    // Absolute address (global/static/const) -> native 16-bit `lda abs`/`sta abs`.
+    if (auto AbsOp = matchAbsoluteAddressing(MRI, Ptr)) {
+      const bool ByteStore =
+          !IsLoad && isByteBuiltStoreValue(cast<GStore>(MI).getValueReg());
+      if (!AllUsesUnmerge && !ByteStore) {
+        Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+        Builder
+            .buildInstr(IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD16_ABS : MOS::G_STORE16_ABS)
+            .add(MI.getOperand(0)) // dst (load) / value (store)
+            .add(*AbsOp)
+            .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+        // B1: under +mos-xy16, constrain the load result to Xc16 when ALL uses
+        // are X-register-compatible (compare, ±1 inc/dec, store-abs, PHI/COPY
+        // chains). This lets selectXY16 emit LDXAbs16 instead of the A16 round-
+        // trip; a misclassification can only miss a win, never add a copy.
+        if (IsLoad && STI.hasIndex16() && !MRI.use_nodbg_empty(Dst)) {
+          SmallSet<Register, 8> Visited;
+          if (allUsesAreXY16Compatible(Dst, MRI, Visited))
+            MRI.setRegClass(Dst, &MOS::Xc16RegClass);
+        }
+        MI.eraseFromParent();
+        return true;
+      }
+      // Byte-only loads and byte-built stores use the narrowing below.
+    } else if (tryIndexedAddressing16(Helper, MRI, MI)) {
+      // abs,x or (zp),y indexed 16-bit load/store (#321 Increment 1e).
+      return true;
+    } else if (STI.has65C02()) {
+      if (isByteIndirectStore())
+        return Helper.narrowScalar(MI, 0, LLT::scalar(8)) ==
+               LegalizerHelper::Legalized;
+      // Runtime 16-bit pointer -> native 16-bit `lda (zp)`/`sta (zp)`.
+      Builder.buildInstr(IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD16_INDIR : MOS::G_STORE16_INDIR)
+          .add(MI.getOperand(0))
+          .addUse(Ptr)
+          .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+      MI.eraseFromParent();
+      return true;
+    }
+  }
+  // zp-pointer / far / non-a16: fall back to the 8-bit byte-pair lowering.
+  return Helper.narrowScalar(MI, 0, LLT::scalar(8)) ==
+         LegalizerHelper::Legalized;
+}

   Helper.Observer.changedInstr(MI);
+  // Select a far byte address while its G_PTR_ADD and zero-extended index are

+// #320 packed-24: legalize a load/store whose VALUE is a packed far pointer (p3,
+// 24-bit, datalayout p3:24:8) as a 3-byte memory access bridged to/from p3 via a
+// 3x s8 merge/unmerge. The bridge merge/unmerge is folded away by the artifact
+// combiner against the matching unmerge/merge in the adjacent addrspacecast (or an
+// adjacent packed load/store for a packed->packed copy), so no 24-bit value or
+// pointer ever reaches the selector (there is no MVT::i24 / 24-bit register class).
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::legalizePackedPtrAccess(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                               MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                               GLoadStore &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  MachineFunction &MF = Builder.getMF();
+  const LLT S8 = LLT::scalar(8);
+  Register Ptr = MI.getPointerReg();
+  LLT PtrTy = MRI.getType(Ptr);
+  LLT OffTy = LLT::scalar(PtrTy.getSizeInBits());
+  const MachineMemOperand &MMO = **MI.memoperands_begin();
+  constexpr unsigned NumBytes = 3;
+
+  // Byte address: base, base+1, base+2.
+  auto byteAddr = [&](unsigned I) -> Register {
+    if (I == 0)
+      return Ptr;
+    return Builder.buildPtrAdd(PtrTy, Ptr, Builder.buildConstant(OffTy, I))
+        .getReg(0);
+  };
+
+  if (auto *Store = dyn_cast<GStore>(&MI)) {
+    auto Bytes = Builder.buildUnmerge(S8, Store->getValueReg()); // p3 -> 3x s8
+    for (unsigned I = 0; I < NumBytes; ++I)
+      Builder.buildStore(Bytes.getReg(I), byteAddr(I),
+                         *MF.getMachineMemOperand(&MMO, I, S8));
+  } else {
+    auto &Load = cast<GLoad>(MI);
+    SmallVector<Register, NumBytes> Bytes;
+    for (unsigned I = 0; I < NumBytes; ++I)
+      Bytes.push_back(Builder
+                          .buildLoad(S8, byteAddr(I),
+                                     *MF.getMachineMemOperand(&MMO, I, S8))
+                          .getReg(0));
+    Builder.buildMergeValues(Load.getDstReg(), Bytes); // 3x s8 -> p3
+  }
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
   return true;
 }
