+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::tryIndexedAddressing16(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                              MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                              GLoadStore &MI) const {
+  LLT S8 = LLT::scalar(8);
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  const MOSSubtarget &STI = Builder.getMF().getSubtarget<MOSSubtarget>();
+
+  if (!STI.hasAccum16() || !STI.has65C02())
+    return false;
+
+  const bool IsLoad = isa<GLoad>(MI);
+  Register Addr = MI.getPointerReg();
+  int64_t ConstOffset = 0;
+  Register VarIndex = 0;
+  // B2: set when a direct s16 offset (16-bit index) is found; switches the
+  // emitted opcode from G_LOAD16_ABS_IDX (8-bit X) to G_LOAD_ABS_IDX16 (16-bit X).
+  bool Use16BitIdx = false;
+
+  // Returns the abs-indexed or indir-indexed opcode based on data width and index width.
+  auto AbsIdxOpc  = [&]() -> unsigned {
+    return Use16BitIdx ? (IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD_ABS_IDX16   : MOS::G_STORE_ABS_IDX16)
+                       : (IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD16_ABS_IDX    : MOS::G_STORE16_ABS_IDX);
+  };
+  auto IndirIdxOpc = [&]() -> unsigned {
+    return Use16BitIdx ? (IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD_INDIR_IDX16  : MOS::G_STORE_INDIR_IDX16)
+                       : (IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD16_INDIR_IDX   : MOS::G_STORE16_INDIR_IDX);
+  };
+
+  while (true) {
+    // Absolute base cases — require a VarIndex already found.
+    if (auto ConstAddr = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(Addr, MRI)) {
+      if (!VarIndex)
+        return false;
+      int64_t Base = ConstAddr->Value.getSExtValue() + ConstOffset;
+      unsigned Opc = AbsIdxOpc();
+      Builder.buildInstr(Opc)
+          .add(MI.getOperand(0))
+          .addImm(Base)
+          .addUse(VarIndex)
+          .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+      MI.eraseFromParent();
+      return true;
+    }
+    if (const MachineInstr *GVAddr = getOpcodeDef(G_GLOBAL_VALUE, Addr, MRI)) {
+      if (!VarIndex)
+        return false;
+      const MachineOperand &GV = GVAddr->getOperand(1);
+      Builder.buildInstr(AbsIdxOpc())
+          .add(MI.getOperand(0))
+          .addGlobalAddress(GV.getGlobal(), GV.getOffset() + ConstOffset)
+          .addUse(VarIndex)
+          .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+      MI.eraseFromParent();
+      return true;
+    }
+    if (const MachineInstr *FIAddr = getOpcodeDef(G_FRAME_INDEX, Addr, MRI)) {
+      const MachineOperand &FI = FIAddr->getOperand(1);
+      if (!VarIndex || !willBeStaticallyAllocated(FI))
+        return false;
+      Builder.buildInstr(AbsIdxOpc())
+          .add(MI.getOperand(0))
+          .addFrameIndex(FI.getIndex(), FI.getOffset() + ConstOffset)
+          .addUse(VarIndex)
+          .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+      MI.eraseFromParent();
+      return true;
+    }
+    // Peel a G_PTR_ADD: constant offsets accumulate; a single 8-bit variable
+    // becomes VarIndex (first one wins; a second means we can't fold both).
+    if (const auto *PtrAdd =
+            cast_if_present<GPtrAdd>(getOpcodeDef(G_PTR_ADD, Addr, MRI))) {
+      Addr = PtrAdd->getBaseReg();
+      Register Off = PtrAdd->getOffsetReg();
+      if (auto C = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(Off, MRI)) {
+        ConstOffset += C->Value.getSExtValue();
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (MachineInstr *ZExt = getOpcodeDef(G_ZEXT, Off, MRI)) {
+        // Pre-legalization: explicit G_ZEXT from a ≤8-bit source.
+        if (VarIndex)
+          return false;
+        Register Src = ZExt->getOperand(1).getReg();
+        LLT SrcTy = MRI.getType(Src);
+        if (SrcTy.getSizeInBits() > 8)
+          return false;
+        if (SrcTy.getSizeInBits() < 8)
+          Src = Builder.buildZExt(S8, Src).getReg(0);
+        VarIndex = Src;
+        continue;
+      }
+      // G_ZEXTLOAD(s16, ptr): the result is a zero-extending load of an ≤8-bit
+      // memory operand.  Using G_TRUNC to s8 here is safe because after the
+      // G_ZEXTLOAD legalizes to G_MERGE_VALUES(lo_s8, G_CONSTANT(0)), the
+      // LegalizationArtifactCombiner reduces the G_TRUNC to lo_s8 directly
+      // (a standalone s8 register).  Crucially, the G_CONSTANT(0) high byte IS
+      // explicitly materialized by the legalizer — unlike a "known-zero" from
+      // bounds analysis, which the RA can elide, leaving the pair's high byte
+      // undefined and crashing the spill-around-call path (seed 56 bug).
+      if (MachineInstr *ZExtLoad = getOpcodeDef(G_ZEXTLOAD, Off, MRI)) {
+        if (VarIndex)
+          return false;
+        LLT LoadTy = ZExtLoad->memoperands().front()->getMemoryType();
+        if (!LoadTy.isScalar() || LoadTy.getSizeInBits() > 8)
+          return false;
+        VarIndex = Builder.buildZExtOrTrunc(S8, Off).getReg(0);
+        continue;
+      }
+      // B2: under +mos-xy16, a direct s16 offset uses the widened index form.
+      // Use16BitIdx switches the emitted opcodes to G_LOAD_ABS_IDX16 etc.
+      if (STI.hasIndex16() && !VarIndex && MRI.getType(Off) == LLT::scalar(16)) {
+        VarIndex = Off;
+        Use16BitIdx = true;
+        continue;
+      }
+      return false;
+    }
+    // Non-absolute base (runtime ZP pointer).
+    // Case 1: variable index, zero accumulated const → `lda (zp),y` with VarIndex.
+    // Case 2: no variable index, const offset in [1,255] → materialise as Y.
+    // Mixed (VarIndex + ConstOffset != 0) can't be expressed in one indexed op.
+    if (VarIndex && ConstOffset == 0) {
+      Builder.buildInstr(IndirIdxOpc())
+          .add(MI.getOperand(0))
+          .addUse(Addr)
+          .addUse(VarIndex)
+          .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+      MI.eraseFromParent();
+      return true;
+    }
+    if (!VarIndex && ConstOffset >= 1 && ConstOffset <= 255 && !Use16BitIdx) {
+      Register Idx = Builder.buildConstant(S8, ConstOffset).getReg(0);
+      Builder.buildInstr(IndirIdxOpc())
+          .add(MI.getOperand(0))
+          .addUse(Addr)
+          .addUse(Idx)
+          .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+      MI.eraseFromParent();
+      return true;
+    }
+    return false;
+  }
+  return false;
+}
+
 bool MOSLegalizerInfo::selectAddressingMode(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
                                             MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
                                             GLoadStore &MI) const {
@@ -1685,6 +2728,20 @@ bool MOSLegalizerInfo::selectAddressingMode(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
       return true;
     return selectIndirectAddressing(Helper, MRI, MI);
   }
+  case 32: {
+    // #320: 32-bit far pointer. A compile-time-constant/global far address uses
+    // 65816 absolute-long (`lda $xxxxxx`); a RUNTIME far pointer uses indirect-
+    // long (`lda [dp]`) through a ZP Imag32 quad.
+    if (tryFarAbsoluteAddressing(Helper, MRI, MI))
+      return true;
+    if (tryFarAbsoluteIndexedAddressing(Helper, MRI, MI))
+      return true;
+    // #321 Phase 2 inc 1: a small constant displacement off that runtime
+    // pointer folds into `lda [dp],y`/`sta [dp],y` instead of a 32-bit add.
+    if (tryFarIndirectIndexedAddressing(Helper, MRI, MI))
+      return true;
+    return tryFarIndirectAddressing(Helper, MRI, MI);
+  }
   default:
     llvm_unreachable("unknown pointer size");
   }
@@ -1750,6 +2807,392 @@ bool MOSLegalizerInfo::tryAbsoluteAddressing(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
   return false;
 }
 
+// #320: select 65816 absolute-long addressing for a 32-bit far pointer whose
+// address is a compile-time constant or (far) global. Replaces the generic
+// G_LOAD/G_STORE with G_LOAD_FAR_ABS/G_STORE_FAR_ABS carrying the 24-bit
+// address operand; the instruction selector lowers those to LDA/STA
+// AbsoluteLong. Returns false for non-absolute far addresses (e.g. a runtime
+// far pointer needing indirect-long) so legalization fails loudly.
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::tryFarAbsoluteAddressing(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                                MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                                GLoadStore &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+
+  unsigned Opcode = isa<GLoad>(MI) ? MOS::G_LOAD_FAR_ABS : MOS::G_STORE_FAR_ABS;
+  auto Operand = matchAbsoluteAddressing(MRI, MI.getPointerReg());
+
+  if (!Operand.has_value())
+    return false;
+
+  Helper.Observer.changingInstr(MI);
+  MI.setDesc(Builder.getTII().get(Opcode));
+  MI.removeOperand(1);
+  MI.addOperand(*Operand);
+  Helper.Observer.changedInstr(MI);
+  return true;
+}
+
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::tryFarAbsoluteIndexedAddressing(
+    LegalizerHelper &Helper, MachineRegisterInfo &MRI, GLoadStore &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  Register Addr = MI.getPointerReg();
+  int64_t Displacement = 0;
+  Register Index;
+  bool WideIndex = false;
+  const MOSSubtarget &STI = Builder.getMF().getSubtarget<MOSSubtarget>();
+
+  while (const auto *Add =
+             cast_if_present<GPtrAdd>(getOpcodeDef(G_PTR_ADD, Addr, MRI))) {
+    Addr = Add->getBaseReg();
+    Register Offset = Add->getOffsetReg();
+    if (auto C = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(Offset, MRI)) {
+      Displacement += C->Value.getSExtValue();
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (Index)
+      return false;
+    if (const MachineInstr *Shift = getOpcodeDef(G_SHL, Offset, MRI)) {
+      auto Amount = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(
+          Shift->getOperand(2).getReg(), MRI);
+      if (!STI.hasIndex16() || !Amount || !Amount->Value.isOne())
+        return false;
+      Register Unscaled = Shift->getOperand(1).getReg();
+      const MachineInstr *Ext = getOpcodeDef(G_ZEXT, Unscaled, MRI);
+      const MachineInstr *Load = getOpcodeDef(G_ZEXTLOAD, Unscaled, MRI);
+      if (Ext && MRI.getType(Ext->getOperand(1).getReg()) == LLT::scalar(8))
+        Unscaled = Ext->getOperand(1).getReg();
+      else if (Load && Load->memoperands().front()->getMemoryType() ==
+                           LLT::scalar(8))
+        Unscaled = Builder.buildZExtOrTrunc(LLT::scalar(8), Unscaled).getReg(0);
+      else
+        return false;
+      Register Wide = Builder.buildZExt(LLT::scalar(16), Unscaled).getReg(0);
+      Index = Builder.buildShl(LLT::scalar(16), Wide,
+                               Builder.buildConstant(LLT::scalar(16), 1))
+                  .getReg(0);
+      WideIndex = true;
+      continue;
+    }
+    const MachineInstr *ZExt = getOpcodeDef(G_ZEXT, Offset, MRI);
+    Register Source;
+    LLT SourceType;
+    if (ZExt) {
+      Source = ZExt->getOperand(1).getReg();
+      SourceType = MRI.getType(Source);
+    } else if (const MachineInstr *Load =
+                   getOpcodeDef(G_ZEXTLOAD, Offset, MRI)) {
+      SourceType = Load->memoperands().front()->getMemoryType();
+      if (SourceType != LLT::scalar(8) && SourceType != LLT::scalar(16))
+        return false;
+      Source = Builder.buildZExtOrTrunc(SourceType, Offset).getReg(0);
+    } else
+      return false;
+    // X contributes an unsigned 8-bit offset in the ambient mode. A full
+    // 16-bit offset requires the +mos-xy16 index-width bracket.
+    if (SourceType == LLT::scalar(8))
+      Index = Source;
+    else if (SourceType == LLT::scalar(16) && STI.hasIndex16()) {
+      Index = Source;
+      WideIndex = true;
+    } else
+      return false;
+  }
+
+  if (!Index)
+    return false;
+  auto Base = matchAbsoluteAddressing(MRI, Addr);
+  if (!Base)
+    return false;
+  if (Base->isImm())
+    Base->setImm(Base->getImm() + Displacement);
+  else if (Base->isGlobal())
+    Base->setOffset(Base->getOffset() + Displacement);
+  else
+    return false;
+
+  bool Wide = MRI.getType(MI.getOperand(0).getReg()) == LLT::scalar(16);
+  unsigned Opcode =
+      Wide ? (WideIndex ? (isa<GLoad>(MI) ? MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS_IDX16
+                                           : MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_ABS_IDX16)
+                        : (isa<GLoad>(MI) ? MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_ABS_IDX
+                                          : MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_ABS_IDX))
+           : (WideIndex ? (isa<GLoad>(MI) ? MOS::G_LOAD_FAR_ABS_IDX16
+                                           : MOS::G_STORE_FAR_ABS_IDX16)
+                        : (isa<GLoad>(MI) ? MOS::G_LOAD_FAR_ABS_IDX
+                                          : MOS::G_STORE_FAR_ABS_IDX));
+  Builder.buildInstr(Opcode)
+      .add(MI.getOperand(0))
+      .add(*Base)
+      .addUse(Index)
+      .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+// #320 far runtime: select 65816 indirect-long addressing for a RUNTIME far
+// pointer — one not matchable as a constant/global, so absolute-long can't be
+// used. Replaces the generic G_LOAD/G_STORE with G_LOAD_FAR_INDIR/
+// G_STORE_FAR_INDIR carrying the far pointer itself; the selector + the Imag32
+// operand class place its 32 bits into a contiguous ZP quad that `lda [dp]` /
+// `sta [dp]` dereferences (reading the low 3 bytes as the 24-bit address).
+// Mirrors how the 16-bit indirect path hands its pointer to G_LOAD_INDIR.
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::tryFarIndirectAddressing(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                                MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                                GLoadStore &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+
+  unsigned Opcode =
+      isa<GLoad>(MI) ? MOS::G_LOAD_FAR_INDIR : MOS::G_STORE_FAR_INDIR;
+  Builder.buildInstr(Opcode)
+      .add(MI.getOperand(0))      // dst (load) / value (store)
+      .addUse(MI.getPointerReg()) // far pointer -> Imag32 ZP quad
+      .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+// Fold a byte displacement of 1..3 from a runtime far pointer into the
+// 65816's [dp],Y addressing mode. Y contains an s8 constant, and the effective
+// address carries into the bank byte. The base remains in an Imag32 ZP quad.
+// Zero displacement needs no index; a link-time base uses absolute-long
+// addressing. Runtime offsets remain part of the materialized base pointer.
+// The small window targets adjacent bytes of scalar loads and stores without
+// requiring range analysis or widening the index register.
+static constexpr int64_t kMaxFarIndirIdxDisp = 3;
+
+// Legalization may turn generic arithmetic, memory operations and intrinsics
+// into calls. Conservatively reject folds that would extend the index's live
+// range across such an instruction.
+static bool mayBecomeCall(const MachineInstr &MI,
+                          const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI) {
+  if (MI.isCall() || isPreISelGenericFloatingPointOpcode(MI.getOpcode()))
+    return true;
+  switch (MI.getOpcode()) {
+  case G_MUL: case G_SMULH: case G_UMULH: case G_SMULO: case G_UMULO:
+  case G_SDIV: case G_UDIV: case G_SREM: case G_UREM:
+  case G_SDIVREM: case G_UDIVREM:
+  case G_MEMCPY: case G_MEMCPY_INLINE: case G_MEMMOVE: case G_MEMSET:
+  case G_BZERO:
+  case G_INTRINSIC: case G_INTRINSIC_W_SIDE_EFFECTS:
+  case G_INTRINSIC_CONVERGENT: case G_INTRINSIC_CONVERGENT_W_SIDE_EFFECTS:
+    return true;
+  case G_SHL: case G_LSHR: case G_ASHR:
+  case G_ROTL: case G_ROTR: case G_FSHL: case G_FSHR:
+    return !getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(
+        MI.getOperand(MI.getNumOperands() - 1).getReg(), MRI);
+  default:
+    return false;
+  }
+}
+
+// The base pointer and index must remain live through the access. Accept only
+// a call-free path within one block or into its sole successor-predecessor
+// edge; other control-flow layouts conservatively decline the fold.
+static bool noCallBetween(const MachineInstr &VDef, const MachineInstr &U,
+                          const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI) {
+  const MachineBasicBlock *VB = VDef.getParent(), *UB = U.getParent();
+  auto Clean = [&](MachineBasicBlock::const_iterator I,
+                   MachineBasicBlock::const_iterator E) {
+    for (; I != E; ++I)
+      if (mayBecomeCall(*I, MRI))
+        return false;
+    return true;
+  };
+  if (VB == UB)
+    return Clean(std::next(VDef.getIterator()), U.getIterator());
+  if (UB->pred_size() != 1 || *UB->pred_begin() != VB)
+    return false;
+  return Clean(std::next(VDef.getIterator()), VB->end()) &&
+         Clean(UB->begin(), U.getIterator());
+}
+
+// The pointer add disappears only when every use is a non-atomic access, or
+// a constant non-negative displacement leading exclusively to such accesses.
+// MaxEnd includes each access's final byte. Escapes and selected fallbacks keep
+// the add alive and must reject the entire group.
+static bool allUsesAreFoldableFarAccesses(const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                          const MachineInstr &VDef,
+                                          Register Ptr, uint64_t Disp,
+                                          uint64_t &MaxEnd,
+                                          unsigned &NumAccesses,
+                                          bool FnMayCall, unsigned Depth) {
+  // Bound the walk and the displacement; neither limit can admit a fold that a
+  // deeper/larger walk would reject — hitting one just declines.
+  if (Depth > 4 || Disp > 0xFFFF)
+    return false;
+  for (const MachineInstr &U : MRI.use_nodbg_instructions(Ptr)) {
+    if (const auto *LS = dyn_cast<GLoadStore>(&U)) {
+      if (LS->getPointerReg() != Ptr || !U.hasOneMemOperand() || LS->isAtomic() ||
+          MRI.getType(U.getOperand(0).getReg()) != LLT::scalar(8))
+        return false;
+      if (const auto *St = dyn_cast<GStore>(LS))
+        if (St->getValueReg() == Ptr) // the pointer is the stored VALUE: escapes
+          return false;
+      LocationSize Sz = LS->getMemSize();
+      if (!Sz.hasValue() || Sz.isScalable() || Sz.getValue().getFixedValue() == 0)
+        return false;
+      if (FnMayCall && !noCallBetween(VDef, U, MRI)) // (e)
+        return false;
+      ++NumAccesses;
+      MaxEnd = std::max<uint64_t>(MaxEnd,
+                                  Disp + Sz.getValue().getFixedValue() - 1);
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (const auto *PA = dyn_cast<GPtrAdd>(&U)) {
+      if (PA->getBaseReg() != Ptr)
+        return false;
+      auto C = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(PA->getOffsetReg(), MRI);
+      if (!C || C->Value.isNegative() || C->Value.getActiveBits() > 16)
+        return false;
+      if (!allUsesAreFoldableFarAccesses(MRI, VDef, PA->getReg(0),
+                                         Disp + C->Value.getZExtValue(), MaxEnd,
+                                         NumAccesses, FnMayCall, Depth + 1))
+        return false;
+      continue;
+    }
+    return false; // PHI, compare, ptrtoint, call, already-legalized fallback, …
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+
+// Fold a runtime byte offset only when its known-bits maximum plus the last
+// accessed byte fits Y (8 bits, or 16 bits under +mos-xy16). Use the computed
+// offset value directly, preserving narrow integer wrapping and bank carries.
+// All uses must fold so the 32-bit pointer add disappears. Absolute bases are
+// declined because materializing their pointer quad can increase register
+// pressure. Calls must not separate the add from its accesses; multiple
+// accesses require a call-free function to avoid repeated spilled-base reloads.
+// Prefer an 8-bit Y whenever the remaining users fit it.
+static bool tryFarRuntimeIndexFold(
+    LegalizerHelper &Helper, MachineRegisterInfo &MRI, GLoadStore &MI,
+    Register Addr, int64_t ConstOffset,
+    function_ref<bool(Register)> IsAbsoluteAddress) {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  const MOSSubtarget &STI = Builder.getMF().getSubtarget<MOSSubtarget>();
+
+  // The indexed pseudos below carry one byte. A native word access must retain
+  // its own lowering, and wider siblings must not keep a partially folded add.
+  if (MRI.getType(MI.getOperand(0).getReg()) != LLT::scalar(8))
+    return false;
+
+  // (a)
+  const auto *V = cast_if_present<GPtrAdd>(getOpcodeDef(G_PTR_ADD, Addr, MRI));
+  if (!V || ConstOffset < 0)
+    return false;
+  // (d)
+  if (IsAbsoluteAddress(V->getBaseReg()))
+    return false;
+  Register Off = V->getOffsetReg();
+  const LLT OffTy = MRI.getType(Off);
+  if (!OffTy.isScalar() || OffTy.getSizeInBits() < 8)
+    return false;
+  GISelValueTracking *VT = Helper.getValueTracking();
+  if (!VT)
+    return false;
+
+  // (b) range proof on the offset itself, before the (costlier) user walk.
+  const uint64_t Limit = STI.hasIndex16() ? 0xFFFF : 0xFF;
+  const APInt MaxOffAP = VT->getKnownBits(Off).getMaxValue();
+  if (MaxOffAP.ugt(Limit))
+    return false;
+  const uint64_t MaxOff = MaxOffAP.getZExtValue();
+
+  // (c) every user of V folds, and the furthest byte any of them reads/writes
+  // still satisfies (b).
+  bool FnMayCall = false;
+  for (const MachineBasicBlock &MBB : Builder.getMF())
+    for (const MachineInstr &I : MBB)
+      FnMayCall |= mayBecomeCall(I, MRI);
+  uint64_t MaxEnd = 0;
+  unsigned NumAccesses = 0;
+  if (!allUsesAreFoldableFarAccesses(MRI, *V, Addr, 0, MaxEnd, NumAccesses,
+                                     FnMayCall, 0))
+    return false;
+  // (f)
+  if (NumAccesses > 1 && FnMayCall)
+    return false;
+  if (MaxOff + MaxEnd > Limit)
+    return false;
+  // This access is one of V's users, so its displacement is inside the walk.
+  assert(static_cast<uint64_t>(ConstOffset) <= MaxEnd);
+
+  const bool Wide = MaxOff + MaxEnd > 0xFF;
+  const LLT IdxTy = LLT::scalar(Wide ? 16 : 8);
+  Register Idx = Builder.buildZExtOrTrunc(IdxTy, Off).getReg(0);
+  if (ConstOffset)
+    Idx = Builder.buildAdd(IdxTy, Idx, Builder.buildConstant(IdxTy, ConstOffset))
+              .getReg(0);
+
+  const bool IsLoad = isa<GLoad>(MI);
+  unsigned Opcode =
+      Wide ? (IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD_FAR_INDIR_IDX16 : MOS::G_STORE_FAR_INDIR_IDX16)
+           : (IsLoad ? MOS::G_LOAD_FAR_INDIR_IDX : MOS::G_STORE_FAR_INDIR_IDX);
+  Builder.buildInstr(Opcode)
+      .add(MI.getOperand(0))  // dst (load) / value (store)
+      .addUse(V->getBaseReg()) // far pointer -> Imag32 ZP quad
+      .addUse(Idx)             // proven-in-range byte offset -> Y
+      .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}
+
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::tryFarIndirectIndexedAddressing(
+    LegalizerHelper &Helper, MachineRegisterInfo &MRI, GLoadStore &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+
+  Register Addr = MI.getPointerReg();
+  int64_t ConstOffset = 0;
+
+  // (a) Peel constant-offset G_PTR_ADDs down to the runtime base pointer.
+  // STOP (don't fail) at the first non-constant offset: that G_PTR_ADD is the
+  // runtime far pointer this access hangs off — e.g. `tbl[i]`'s second byte is
+  // G_PTR_ADD(G_PTR_ADD(@tbl, i), 1), and the base we want is the inner node,
+  // which the legalizer materialises into the Imag32 quad exactly as it already
+  // does for the byte-0 access. Failing here instead would miss the single most
+  // common customer (the far array subscript).
+  while (const auto *PtrAdd =
+             cast_if_present<GPtrAdd>(getOpcodeDef(G_PTR_ADD, Addr, MRI))) {
+    auto C = getIConstantVRegValWithLookThrough(PtrAdd->getOffsetReg(), MRI);
+    if (!C)
+      break;
+    ConstOffset += C->Value.getSExtValue();
+    Addr = PtrAdd->getBaseReg();
+  }
+
+  // Fold the range-proven runtime offset and displacement together when the
+  // pointer add can disappear, preserving the offset's integer arithmetic.
+  if (tryFarRuntimeIndexFold(Helper, MRI, MI, Addr, ConstOffset,
+                             [&](Register R) {
+                               return matchAbsoluteAddressing(MRI, R)
+                                   .has_value();
+                             }))
+    return true;
+
+  // (b) Only the zero-proof displacement window.
+  if (ConstOffset < 1 || ConstOffset > kMaxFarIndirIdxDisp)
+    return false;
+
+  // (c) Defensive: never steal a base that absolute-long addressing could take.
+  // The caller's tryFarAbsoluteAddressing already ruled this out; re-checking
+  // here keeps the predicate true independently of call order.
+  if (matchAbsoluteAddressing(MRI, Addr).has_value())
+    return false;
+
+  bool Wide = MRI.getType(MI.getOperand(0).getReg()) == LLT::scalar(16);
+  unsigned Opcode = Wide ? (isa<GLoad>(MI) ? MOS::G_LOAD16_FAR_INDIR_IDX
+                                         : MOS::G_STORE16_FAR_INDIR_IDX)
+                         : (isa<GLoad>(MI) ? MOS::G_LOAD_FAR_INDIR_IDX
+                                           : MOS::G_STORE_FAR_INDIR_IDX);
+  Register Idx = Builder.buildConstant(LLT::scalar(8), ConstOffset).getReg(0);
+  Builder.buildInstr(Opcode)
+      .add(MI.getOperand(0)) // dst (load) / value (store)
+      .addUse(Addr)          // runtime far pointer -> Imag32 ZP quad
+      .addUse(Idx)           // displacement -> Y
+      .addMemOperand(*MI.memoperands_begin());
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}

+        // generic register-replacement helpers do. The users can be CSE-able
+        // (G_TRUNC, G_PTR_ADD, G_ADD ...) and GISelCSEInfo keys its FoldingSet

+        // stale node whose bucket is the OLD profile's hash, so a later lookup
+        // for the NEW form (`G_TRUNC Explicit16` from selectIndirectAddressing's

+        // i.e. on heap layout. That is a non-reproducible build (the `sec`
+        // placement flip in `_title_blank`, ~0.5 % of builds). The notification
+        // also queues the users for re-legalization, which is the contract for
+        // any operand change.
+        MachineInstr *TruncMI = MRI.getVRegDef(Lo);
+        for (MachineOperand &MO :
+             llvm::make_early_inc_range(MRI.use_operands(NewOffset))) {
+          MachineInstr &UseMI = *MO.getParent();
+          if (&UseMI == TruncMI)
+            continue;
+          Helper.Observer.changingInstr(UseMI);
+          MO.setReg(Explicit16);
+          Helper.Observer.changedInstr(UseMI);
+        }
+
+        Builder.setInsertPt(SavedMBB, SavedInsertPt);
+        Index = Lo;
+        continue;
+      }
+      // B2: under +mos-xy16, a direct s16 offset uses the widened form.
+      if (STI.hasIndex16() && !Index && MRI.getType(NewOffset) == LLT::scalar(16)) {
+        Opcode = isa<GLoad>(MI) ? MOS::G_LOAD_ABS_IDX16 : MOS::G_STORE_ABS_IDX16;
+        Index = NewOffset;

       }
     }

+// #320: does any *pointer* operand of a mem-op live in a far address space? The
+// operands are {dst, val|src, len, tail-imm}; the trailing tail-call imm is
+// skipped. AS_FarPacked never reaches a mem-op as the access pointer (it is
+// value-only and cast to AS_Far before any deref — see the G_LOAD/G_STORE comment
+// near the top of this file) but is matched defensively.
+static bool anyFarPointerOperand(const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                 const MachineInstr &MI) {
+  for (unsigned I = 0, E = MI.getNumOperands() - 1; I < E; ++I) {
+    const MachineOperand &MO = MI.getOperand(I);
+    if (!MO.isReg())
+      continue;
+    LLT Ty = MRI.getType(MO.getReg());
+    if (Ty.isPointer() && (Ty.getAddressSpace() == MOS::AS_Far ||
+                           Ty.getAddressSpace() == MOS::AS_FarPacked))
+      return true;
+  }
+  return false;
+}
+
+// #320: a far-aware mirror of LegalizerHelper::createMemLibcall. The generic
+// createMemLibcall would call the NEAR runtime (memcpy / __memset, a 16-bit
+// char*) while passing the 32-bit far pointer through the Imag32 far ABI — the
+// callee reads only the 16-bit offset, silently dropping the bank byte (a
+// wrong-bank access). Instead call the far runtime (__memset_far / __memcpy_far /
+// __memmove_far) and materialize every pointer arg as a far (addrspace 2) value:
+// any near/zp/packed pointer is widened with G_ADDRSPACE_CAST (near->far
+// zero-extends bank $00, correct under the DBR=0 model), so one far runtime
+// function per op covers far<-far, far<-near and near<-far in a single call.
+static bool createFarMemLibcall(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                MachineRegisterInfo &MRI, MachineInstr &MI) {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  auto &Ctx = Builder.getMF().getFunction().getContext();
+  const LLT PF = LLT::pointer(MOS::AS_Far, 32);
+
+  unsigned Opc = MI.getOpcode();
+  const char *Callee;
+  switch (Opc) {
+  case MOS::G_MEMSET:
+    Callee = "__memset_far";
+    break;
+  case MOS::G_MEMCPY:
+    Callee = "__memcpy_far";
+    break;
+  case MOS::G_MEMMOVE:
+    Callee = "__memmove_far";
+    break;
+  default:
+    return false;
+  }
+  const bool IsSet = Opc == MOS::G_MEMSET;
+
+  // Build args by role. Operands are {dst, val|src, len, tail-imm}.
+  // A near/zp/packed pointer is widened to far (bank $00) so the runtime's far
+  // params match; a far pointer passes through unchanged.
+  auto asFar = [&](Register Reg) {
+    if (MRI.getType(Reg).getAddressSpace() != MOS::AS_Far)
+      Reg = Builder.buildAddrSpaceCast(PF, Reg).getReg(0);
+    return Reg;
+  };
+  Type *FarPtrTy = PointerType::get(Ctx, MOS::AS_Far);
+
+  SmallVector<CallLowering::ArgInfo, 3> Args;
+  // arg 0: destination pointer.
+  Args.push_back({asFar(MI.getOperand(0).getReg()), FarPtrTy, 0});
+  if (IsSet) {
+    // arg 1: fill byte (i8, matching __memset's `char` value).
+    Register Val = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
+    Args.push_back(
+        {Val, IntegerType::get(Ctx, MRI.getType(Val).getSizeInBits()), 1});
+  } else {
+    // arg 1: source pointer.
+    Args.push_back({asFar(MI.getOperand(1).getReg()), FarPtrTy, 1});
+  }
+  // arg 2: length, coerced to size_t (16-bit on MOS). The loop-idiom recognizer
+  // forms a far memset with a 32-bit length (the far index width); narrow it so
+  // the ABI exactly matches the runtime's size_t parameter rather than relying on
+  // low-word register placement. The value always fits (size_t <= 0xFFFF).
+  Register Len = MI.getOperand(2).getReg();
+  unsigned LenBits = MRI.getType(Len).getSizeInBits();
+  if (LenBits > 16)
+    Len = Builder.buildTrunc(LLT::scalar(16), Len).getReg(0);
+  else if (LenBits < 16)
+    Len = Builder.buildZExt(LLT::scalar(16), Len).getReg(0);
+  Args.push_back({Len, IntegerType::get(Ctx, 16), 2});
+
+  auto &CLI = *Builder.getMF().getSubtarget().getCallLowering();
+  CallLowering::CallLoweringInfo Info;
+  Info.CallConv = CallingConv::C; // CC_MOS; the addrspace-2 args select Imag32.
+  Info.Callee = MachineOperand::CreateES(Callee);
+  Info.OrigRet = CallLowering::ArgInfo({0}, Type::getVoidTy(Ctx), 0);
+  // The far runtime returns void; nothing consumes a return, so there is no tail
+  // call and no "returned" arg coupling (unlike the near createMemLibcall).
+  Info.IsTailCall = false;
+  llvm::append_range(Info.OrigArgs, Args);
+  if (!CLI.lowerCall(Builder, Info))
+    return false;
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}

+  // #320: the inline path above already lowers a far memop correctly (per-byte
+  // far G_STOREs) for a constant size <= SizeLimit. When it bails (variable size,
+  // or constant size over the limit) we must NOT fall into the generic

+// __builtin_frame_address(0) is the incoming soft stack pointer: the address
+// just above this function's soft-stack frame, where its stack arguments begin.
+// A fixed object at offset zero names exactly that address, so the existing
+// frame-index lowering produces it and no frame pointer is needed.
+//
+// __builtin_return_address(0) is the word JSR/JSL pushed on the hard stack plus
+// one: both push the address of their own last byte, and RTS/RTL add one on the
+// way back. SPC700 CALL pushes the return address itself. How deep that word
+// sits below the stack pointer at the point of the read is not known until
+// frame lowering and register scavenging are done, so the read stays where it
+// is as a pseudo and MOSLowerReturnAddress expands it last, once every push is
+// in place.
+//
+// Neither stack carries a frame chain, so levels above zero are zero, which is
+// what SelectionDAG's generic expansion of these nodes returns. An interrupt
+// handler has no C return address (the interrupted PC sits below P, and the
+// 65816 handler prologue pushes more), so it returns zero as well.
+bool MOSLegalizerInfo::legalizeReturnOrFrameAddress(LegalizerHelper &Helper,
+                                                    MachineInstr &MI) const {
+  MachineIRBuilder &Builder = Helper.MIRBuilder;
+  MachineFunction &MF = *MI.getMF();
+  MachineFrameInfo &MFI = MF.getFrameInfo();
+  const MOSSubtarget &STI = MF.getSubtarget<MOSSubtarget>();
+  Register Dst = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
+  bool IsReturnAddress =
+      cast<GIntrinsic>(MI).getIntrinsicID() == Intrinsic::returnaddress;
+  int64_t Level = MI.getOperand(2).getImm();
+  LLT P = LLT::pointer(0, 16);
+  LLT S8 = LLT::scalar(8);
+  LLT S16 = LLT::scalar(16);
+
+  if (Level != 0 || (IsReturnAddress && STI.getFrameLowering()->isISR(MF))) {
+    Builder.buildConstant(Dst, 0);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  if (!IsReturnAddress) {
+    int FI = MFI.CreateFixedObject(1, 0, /*IsImmutable=*/true);
+    Builder.buildFrameIndex(Dst, FI);
+    MI.eraseFromParent();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  MFI.setReturnAddressIsTaken(true);
+  auto Lo = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::G_RETURN_ADDRESS_BYTE, {S8}, {}).addImm(0);
+  auto Hi = Builder.buildInstr(MOS::G_RETURN_ADDRESS_BYTE, {S8}, {}).addImm(1);
+  auto Pushed = Builder.buildIntToPtr(
+      P, Builder.buildMergeLikeInstr(S16, {Lo.getReg(0), Hi.getReg(0)}));
+  if (STI.hasSPC700())
+    Builder.buildCopy(Dst, Pushed);
+  else
+    Builder.buildPtrAdd(Dst, Pushed, Builder.buildConstant(S16, 1));
+  MI.eraseFromParent();
+  return true;
+}

-static void assertNZDeadAt(MachineBasicBlock &MBB,

+// Liveness immediately before Pos (i.e. into the instruction at Pos), computed
+// by stepping a basic-block live-out set backward to Pos.
+static void computeLiveBefore(MachineBasicBlock::iterator Pos,
+                              LivePhysRegs &LPR) {
+  MachineBasicBlock &MBB = *Pos->getParent();
+  LPR.addLiveOuts(MBB);
+  for (MachineBasicBlock::iterator J = std::prev(MBB.end()); J != Pos; --J)
+    LPR.stepBackward(*J);
+  LPR.stepBackward(*Pos);
+}
+
+// Find an 8-bit index register (X or Y) that is dead immediately before Pos, so
+// it can serve as a transient courier to move the processor-status byte through
+// the hard stack. X/Y are width-safe here: MOSInsertREPSEP runs after register
+// scavenging (addPreEmitPass) and classifies any push/pull/load/store of an
+// index register as XW_X8, so it will bracket these with sep/rep as needed even
+// under +mos-xy16. Returns MOS::NoRegister if neither is free.
+static Register findDeadIndexReg(MachineBasicBlock::iterator Pos) {
+  const MachineFunction &MF = *Pos->getParent()->getParent();
+  const TargetRegisterInfo &TRI = *MF.getSubtarget().getRegisterInfo();
+  const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = MF.getRegInfo();
+  // Without accurate liveness (e.g. a MIR test without tracksRegLiveness) no
+  // index register can be proven dead; the caller then has only the balanced
+  // hard-stack path.
+  if (!MF.getProperties().hasTracksLiveness())
+    return MOS::NoRegister;
+  LivePhysRegs LPR(TRI);
+  computeLiveBefore(Pos, LPR);
+  for (Register R : {MOS::X, MOS::Y})
+    if (LPR.available(MRI, R.asMCReg()))
+      return R;
+  return MOS::NoRegister;
+}
+
+// Does no part of Reg hold an available value on entry to Pos? The scavenger
+// borrows $c (a sub-register of $p) for a frame-index carry, which forces the
+// whole $p to be preserved even where $p carries no live value; the resulting
+// PHP then reads an undef $p, which the machine verifier rejects unless the
+// operand is flagged undef.
+//
+// This must mirror the verifier's own notion of "defined" exactly, because that
+// is the thing being satisfied. MachineVerifier tracks a FORWARD availability
+// set (block live-ins, plus each def, minus each kill/dead operand) and accepts
+// a use of a composite register when *any* of its sub-registers is in that set
+// (`We are fine if just any subregister has a defined value`). So the predicate
+// is "no sub-register of $p is available here", not "no def of $p appears
+// above": $c may well be defined earlier in the block and then killed or
+// dead-flagged before Pos, which leaves $p wholly undefined at Pos even though
+// a reaching-definition scan finds a modifier. That is precisely the shape a
+// 16-bit-accumulator ALU chain produces — every ADC in the chain defines $c,
+// and the last one marks it dead — and it is what a reaching-def test missed.
+//
+// Note this is deliberately *not* a backward-liveness test: $p is "live" by
+// backward dataflow at this very point (this PHP uses it), so LivePhysRegs
+// stepped backward would never report it free. Forward availability is both the
+// verifier-faithful and the semantically correct question here — if nothing
+// above produced a flag value, the byte this PHP pushes is undefined, and the
+// matching PLP restores an undefined byte that nothing reads.
+//
+// Erring is one-sided: reporting "available" when nothing is leaves the operand
+// un-flagged (a verifier complaint, never a miscompile), whereas a spurious
+// `undef` on a genuinely live $p would let the flag def move or die. Matching
+// the verifier's set exactly keeps that asymmetry intact.
+static bool hasNoAvailableValue(Register Reg, MachineBasicBlock::iterator Pos) {
+  MachineBasicBlock &MBB = *Pos->getParent();
+  const MachineFunction &MF = *MBB.getParent();
+  const TargetRegisterInfo &TRI = *MF.getSubtarget().getRegisterInfo();
+  const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = MF.getRegInfo();
+
+  // The verifier only checks physical-register liveness when the function
+  // tracks it, and block live-ins cannot be read otherwise; leave the operand
+  // unflagged in that case (the side that can never miscompile).
+  if (!MF.getProperties().hasTracksLiveness())
+    return false;
+
+  LivePhysRegs LPR(TRI);
+  LPR.addLiveIns(MBB);
+  SmallVector<std::pair<MCPhysReg, const MachineOperand *>, 4> Clobbers;
+  for (MachineBasicBlock::iterator J = MBB.begin(); J != Pos; ++J) {
+    Clobbers.clear();
+    LPR.stepForward(*J, Clobbers);
   }
-  assert(!LiveRegs.contains(MOS::N) &&
-         "expected N to be free when saving scavenger register");
-  assert(!LiveRegs.contains(MOS::Z) &&
-         "expected Z to be free when saving scavenger register");
-#endif
+  // available() is false when any alias (here: any sub-register) of Reg is
+  // live, which is exactly the verifier's "any subregister has a defined
+  // value" escape hatch.
+  return LPR.available(MRI, Reg.asMCReg());
 }

+// #321 xy16: does A16 (or either of its bytes, $a / $b) carry a value that is
+// live across Pos? Pos is an LDStk/STStk, which never names A16 in an operand,
+// so liveness immediately before it equals liveness immediately after it —
+// exactly the "is something depending on the accumulator across this spill"
+// question expandLDSTStk needs to answer.
+//
+// LivePhysRegs::available() is alias-aware, so a live 8-bit $a answers yes too.
+// That is deliberate, not over-approximation: the staging sequence below is
+// `lda (zp)` / `tax` (or `txa` / `sta (zp)`), which destroys $a just as
+// thoroughly as it destroys $a16.
+//
+// Erring is one-sided by construction. A spurious "live" costs one `pha`/`pla`
+// pair (2 bytes, 7 cycles) and is always semantically harmless; a missed "live"
+// is a miscompile. The estimate can only err toward "live": the scan runs during
+// PEI's forward walk over the block, so any LDStk/STStk *below* Pos is still an
+// unexpanded pseudo that names no accumulator — it can therefore only fail to
+// report a kill of A16, never invent a use of it.
+static bool accumulatorLiveAcross(MachineBasicBlock::iterator Pos) {
+  const MachineFunction &MF = *Pos->getParent()->getParent();
+  const TargetRegisterInfo &TRI = *MF.getSubtarget().getRegisterInfo();
+  const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = MF.getRegInfo();
+  LivePhysRegs LPR(TRI);
+  computeLiveBefore(Pos, LPR);
+  return !LPR.available(MRI, Register(MOS::A16).asMCReg());
+}
+
+// #321 xy16: a 16-bit index register has no (zp)-indirect load or store on the
+// 65816, so an X16/Y16 soft-stack spill has to be staged through the 16-bit
+// accumulator — `txa; sta (ptr)` out, `lda (ptr); tax` back (expandLDSTStkImpl
+// below). Neither LDStk nor STStk carries an A16 operand, so that clobber is
+// invisible to register allocation, which will happily leave a live 16-bit value
+// in A16 across the spill and then read it back destroyed. That is the
+// retryjmp_gate_crc miscompile: the value bound for `rj_result[i]` sat in A16
+// across the X16 reload, and the store wrote back the reloaded *index*.
+//
+// Declaring the clobber on the pseudo instead is not an option: these pseudos
+// are created by the spiller during register allocation, after live intervals
+// are built, so a physical-register def added there is not reflected in the
+// regunit live ranges the allocator checks interference against. Worse, Ac16 has
+// exactly one member, so "avoid A16 here" is not something the allocator could
+// satisfy by recolouring — only by spilling, which is precisely what this
+// bracket does, but cheaper and only where it is actually needed.
+//
+// So preserve A16 locally instead, and only when something is live in it. The
+// save must precede the pointer materialization inside the expansion (which
+// scavenges $a for its `clc; lda; adc; sta` chain), and the restore must follow
+// the whole sequence — hence the bracket lives here in the wrapper rather than
+// in the Xc16/Yc16 arms.
+//
+// The Imag32/Imag16 splits inside the body recurse through this wrapper (they are
+// never Xc16/Yc16, so it short-circuits before even computing liveness). The one
+// re-entry that must NOT go through it is the body's own offset-0 restart after
+// materializing a pointer: that is the SAME spill, already bracketed above, and a
+// second bracket there would save an accumulator the address chain has just
+// clobbered.
 void MOSRegisterInfo::expandLDSTStk(MachineBasicBlock::iterator MI) const {
+  const bool IsLoad = MI->getOpcode() == MOS::LDStk;
+  Register Loc =
+      IsLoad ? MI->getOperand(0).getReg() : MI->getOperand(1).getReg();
+  const bool StagesThroughA16 =
+      MOS::Xc16RegClass.contains(Loc) || MOS::Yc16RegClass.contains(Loc);
+
+  if (!StagesThroughA16 || !accumulatorLiveAcross(MI)) {
+    expandLDSTStkImpl(MI);
+    return;
+  }
+
+  MachineBasicBlock &MBB = *MI->getParent();
+  // Captured before the expansion, which inserts ahead of MI and then erases it;
+  // Next therefore still marks the point just past everything it emitted.
+  MachineBasicBlock::iterator Next = std::next(MI);
+  MachineIRBuilder Builder(MBB, MI);
+  Builder.buildInstr(MOS::PHA16).addUse(MOS::A16);
+  expandLDSTStkImpl(MI);
+  Builder.setInsertPt(MBB, Next);
+  Builder.buildInstr(MOS::PLA16).addDef(MOS::A16);
+}
+
+void MOSRegisterInfo::expandLDSTStkImpl(MachineBasicBlock::iterator MI) const {
   MachineFunction &MF = *MI->getMF();
   MachineIRBuilder Builder(*MI);
   MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = *Builder.getMRI();
@@ -427,10 +672,25 @@ void MOSRegisterInfo::expandLDSTStk(MachineBasicBlock::iterator MI) const {
       IsLoad ? MI->getOperand(0).getReg() : MI->getOperand(1).getReg();

+// True if the virtual register Reg's unique definition is a COPY *directly* out of a
+// physical imaginary register ($rcN) — `Reg = COPY $rcN` — and Reg is live across a
+// call that clobbers that same $rcN.
+//
+// $rcN are the calling convention's imaginary (zero-page) argument/return scratch: a
+// libcall passes and returns values in them and clobbers them (via the call's
+// regmask). A `Reg = COPY $rcN` reading such a return value hints Reg back to $rcN.
+// If Reg is then folded into an imaginary-register *pair* (a sub-register copy
+// building an Imag16) that outlives a libcall re-clobbering $rcN, the pair inherits
+// the $rcN hint and the allocator re-binds it to $rcN across the clobber — leaving a
+// use of $rcN with no reaching definition on that path. The value survives in $rcN at
+// runtime so the code is correct, but the IR is ill-formed and the MachineVerifier
+// rejects it ("Using an undefined physical register") — a latent miscompile hazard.
+static bool copiedFromClobberedPhysImag(Register Reg,
+                                        const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI,
+                                        LiveIntervals &LIS) {
+  if (!Reg.isVirtual() || !LIS.hasInterval(Reg))
+    return false;
+  const MachineInstr *Def = MRI.getUniqueVRegDef(Reg);
+  if (!Def || !Def->isCopy())
+    return false;
+  Register Src = Def->getOperand(1).getReg();
+  if (!Src.isPhysical() || !MOS::Imag8RegClass.contains(Src))
+    return false;
+  BitVector Usable;
+  return LIS.checkRegMaskInterference(LIS.getInterval(Reg), Usable) &&
+         !Usable.test(Src.id());
+}
+
 bool MOSRegisterInfo::shouldCoalesce(
     MachineInstr *MI, const TargetRegisterClass *SrcRC, unsigned SubReg,
     const TargetRegisterClass *DstRC, unsigned DstSubReg,
     const TargetRegisterClass *NewRC, LiveIntervals &LIS) const {
   const auto &MRI = MI->getMF()->getRegInfo();
 
+  // Don't fold a value read straight out of a call-clobbered imaginary register
+  // ($rcN, via `vreg = COPY $rcN`) into an imaginary-register *pair* (a sub-register
+  // copy that builds an Imag16) when that value outlives the clobbering call. The
+  // pair inherits the physical-$rcN allocation hint and the allocator re-binds it to
+  // $rcN across the clobber, producing a disconnected def->use the MachineVerifier
+  // rejects ("Using an undefined physical register") — a latent miscompile hazard.
+  // Refusing keeps the COPY so the value gets its own spillable vreg.
+  //
+  // Narrowly gated to stay inert on the common case: it requires (1) a *direct*
+  // `vreg = COPY $rcN` read (unique def), (2) that vreg genuinely live across a call
+  // clobbering $rcN, and (3) a sub-register copy into an Imag16 pair — the exact shape
+  // that re-creates the re-bindable hint. It only ever keeps an extra COPY, never
+  // changing correctness. (A distinct manifestation, where the allocator binds a
+  // *pure-virtual* value — no $rcN copy at all — to a clobbered $rc pair, is an
+  // RA-assignment defect this coalescer guard cannot target; see the plan.)
+  if (NewRC == &MOS::Imag16RegClass && (DstSubReg || SubReg)) {
+    if (copiedFromClobberedPhysImag(MI->getOperand(0).getReg(), MRI, LIS) ||
+        copiedFromClobberedPhysImag(MI->getOperand(1).getReg(), MRI, LIS))
+      return false;
+  }
+
   // Don't coalesce Imag8 and AImag8 registers together when used by shifts or
   // rotates.  This may cause expensive ASL zp's to be used when ASL A would
   // have sufficed. It's better to do arithmetic in A and then copy it out.

+// #321 Increment 1b: the 65816's 16-bit accumulator, modeled as A16 = B:A. WDC
+// calls this combined register "C", but we deliberately do NOT use that name —
+// `def C` (register 7, a subreg of P) is the *carry flag*, and conflating the
+// 16-bit accumulator with carry is a classic 65816 footgun. So the 16-bit
+// accumulator is `A16` (low byte A, high byte B), unrelated to carry.
+//
+// `B` is the accumulator's hidden high byte (swapped with A via XBA), only live
+// as the high half of A16 in native 16-bit-accumulator mode (M=0); 8-bit ops
+// never name it. A16 aliases A as its low byte so the register allocator knows a
+// 16-bit accumulator op clobbers A. Numbers 14/15 are free (reals end at
+// Fake=13, imaginaries start at 0x10). Used only under +mos-a16; inert otherwise.

+// #320 far runtime: 32-bit imaginary registers (consecutive pairs of 16-bit RS
+// words = 4 contiguous zero-page __rc bytes), the ZP home of a runtime far
+// (addrspace 2) pointer dereferenced via `lda [dp]` / `sta [dp]`. Each RL#K is a
+// fixed super-register over RS#(2K):RS#(2K+1), so register allocation guarantees
+// the four bytes are physically adjacent in ZP (the [dp] mode reads dp..dp+2).
+// RL members whose underlying RS words overlap a reserved pointer (RL0 ⊃ RS0 the
+// stack pointer, RL4 ⊃ RS8 the scavenger) or fall beyond the imaginary window
+// are made unallocatable implicitly through their shared __rc register units —
+// no explicit RL reservation (which would wrongly reserve the sibling RS too).
+class MOSImagReg32<bits<16> num, string name, list<Register> subregs>
+    : MOSReg<num, name> {
+  let SubRegs = subregs;
+  let SubRegIndices = [sublo16, subhi16];
+  let CoveredBySubRegs = 1;
+}
+defvar MaxImag32Regs = !sra(MaxImag16Regs, 1);

       Benefit += 12 * RestoreFreq; // LDA ABS,STA ZP
     }
 
+    // #320 far pointers: if the CSR belongs to an Imag32 quad with live uses
+    // (a far pointer read via [dp] indirect-long), all FOUR bytes must be
+    // assigned as one consecutive unit, and the quad super-register itself
+    // must get a CSRZPOffsets entry so an Imag32 operand (e.g. the pointer of
+    // LDA_IndirectLong) is rewritten to the ZP-stack location like its byte
+    // sub-registers are. Without this, the byte defs are silently renamed but
+    // the [dp] use keeps naming the original __rc quad -> reads garbage.
+    const TargetRegisterInfo &TRI = *MF.getSubtarget().getRegisterInfo();
+    Register Imag32 = 0;
+    for (MCPhysReg Super : TRI.superregs(Reg))
+      if (MOS::Imag32RegClass.contains(Super)) {
+        Imag32 = Super;
+        break;
+      }
+    if (Imag32 && !MF.getRegInfo().reg_nodbg_empty(Imag32)) {
+      // Don't create the Imag32 candidate more than once (each of the quad's
+      // four bytes reaches this loop).
+      if (Imag32Regs.contains(Imag32))
+        continue;
+
+      Reg = Imag32;
+      Imag32Regs.insert(Reg);
+
+      // Account for the remaining three bytes.
+      for (int Byte = 0; Byte != 3; ++Byte) {
+        if (Idx++ < 4) {
+          Benefit += 9 * SaveFreq;      // LDA ZP,PHA
+          Benefit += 10 * RestoreFreq;  // PLA,STA ZP
+        } else {
+          Benefit += 12 * SaveFreq;     // LDA ZP,STA ABS
+          Benefit += 12 * RestoreFreq;  // LDA ABS,STA ZP
+        }
+        ++Size;
+      }
+      Benefit /= Size;
+
+      Candidates.push_back(
+          std::make_unique<Candidate>(Candidate{&MF, Size, /*CSR=*/Reg}));
+      LocalCandidates.push_back(
+          LocalCandidate{Candidates.back().get(), Benefit});
+      continue;
+    }
+
